`DEPLOYMENT.md`:

```md
# Ultimo Deployment & Hosting Guide

This guide details how to deploy and host the **Ultimo Server** in production environments, covering **Google Cloud Run (Serverless)**, **Virtual Private Servers (VPS)**, and **Reverse Proxy SSL configurations**.

---

## Architecture Overview

Ultimo features a **Unified Transport Layer**: a single port (default `8080` or dynamically injected via `$PORT`) automatically multiplexes:
- **Raw TCP socket connections** (CLI clients, bots, Netcat).
- **RFC 6455 WebSockets (`ws://` / `wss://`)** for browser clients.
- **HTTP GET requests** serving the embedded single-page HTML5 web client on `GET /` and health checks on `GET /health`.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph Clients["Clients"]
        Browser["Web Browser (Chrome/Firefox/Safari)"]
        CLI["Terminal CLI Client (run-client.bat/.sh)"]
        Netcat["Raw TCP Tools (nc localhost 8080)"]
    end

    subgraph Internet["Public Internet / Ingress"]
        CloudRun["Google Cloud Run / NGINX / Caddy (Port 443 / 80)"]
    end

    subgraph Container["Ultimo Container (Port 8080)"]
        Multiplexer["ClientSession Multiplexer"]
        WebSockets["RFC 6455 WebSocket Engine"]
        TCP["Raw Line TCP Handler"]
        StaticHTTP["Static Web Client & /health"]
        Rooms["Actor-based Game Rooms (Virtual Threads)"]
    end

    Browser -->|"HTTPS / WSS"| CloudRun
    CLI -->|"Raw TCP"| CloudRun
    Netcat -->|"Raw TCP"| CloudRun
    CloudRun --> Multiplexer
    Multiplexer --> WebSockets
    Multiplexer --> TCP
    Multiplexer --> StaticHTTP
    WebSockets --> Rooms
    TCP --> Rooms
```

---

## Option 1: Google Cloud Run (Recommended for FAANG Portfolios)

[Google Cloud Run](https://cloud.google.com/run) is a fully managed serverless platform that runs containers directly on Google's scalable infrastructure.

### Key Advantages
- **Generous Free Tier:** 2 million requests per month for free (effectively $0/month for portfolio projects).
- **Automatic HTTPS & Certificates:** Free SSL/TLS certificate managed by Google (`https://your-service-xyz.a.run.app`).
- **Native WebSocket Support:** Built-in support for long-lived streaming connections and WebSockets.
- **Scale to Zero:** Zero cost when no games are active.

### Deployment Steps

#### 1. Install Google Cloud SDK & Login
```bash
# Authenticate with your Google account
gcloud auth login

# Set your target project ID
gcloud config set project YOUR_PROJECT_ID
```

#### 2. Deploy Using Included Script
Run the automated deployment script from the project root:

- **Linux / macOS:**
  ```bash
  chmod +x cloudrun-deploy.sh
  ./cloudrun-deploy.sh
  ```
- **Windows:**
  ```cmd
  cloudrun-deploy.bat
  ```

#### 3. Manual Deployment Command
```bash
gcloud run deploy ultimo-server \
  --source . \
  --region europe-west1 \
  --allow-unauthenticated \
  --port 8080 \
  --memory 512Mi \
  --cpu 1 \
  --min-instances 0 \
  --max-instances 2 \
  --timeout 3600 \
  --session-affinity
```

> **Why `--session-affinity` and `--timeout 3600`?**  
> Enables sticky routing so all WebSocket frames from a match land on the same container instance, and keeps the socket alive for up to 60 minutes of gameplay.

Once deployed, Google Cloud will output a live URL (e.g. `https://ultimo-server-xyz-ew.a.run.app`). Open it in any browser to play immediately!

---

## Option 2: Linux VPS (Hetzner, DigitalOcean, AWS EC2, OVH)

For dedicated multiplayer hosting where players can connect both via web browser and raw TCP sockets simultaneously on arbitrary ports:

### Requirements
- Any Linux VPS (Ubuntu 22.04+ or Debian 12+) with 1GB RAM (~$3-4/month).
- Docker and Docker Compose installed.

### Steps

#### 1. Clone & Launch Container
```bash
git clone https://github.com/YOUR_USERNAME/Ultimo.git
cd Ultimo
docker compose up -d --build
```

#### 2. Check Service Health
```bash
docker compose ps
docker compose logs -f
```

The server is now live on `http://YOUR_SERVER_IP:8080` (for web browsers) and `YOUR_SERVER_IP:8080` (for raw TCP clients).

---

## Option 3: Production Reverse Proxy with SSL (Caddy / NGINX)

To map a custom domain (e.g., `ultimo.yourdomain.com`) with automated Let's Encrypt SSL certificates:

### A. Using Caddy (Easiest, Auto-SSL)
Create `Caddyfile`:
```caddy
ultimo.yourdomain.com {
    reverse_proxy localhost:8080
}
```
Run Caddy:
```bash
caddy run
```
Caddy automatically provisions SSL certificates and handles WebSocket connection upgrades out of the box.

### B. Using NGINX
```nginx
server {
    listen 80;
    server_name ultimo.yourdomain.com;
    return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
}

server {
    listen 443 ssl http2;
    server_name ultimo.yourdomain.com;

    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/ultimo.yourdomain.com/fullchain.pem;
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/ultimo.yourdomain.com/privkey.pem;

    location / {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_read_timeout 3600s;
        proxy_send_timeout 3600s;
    }
}
```

---

## Local Verification

To test the web and WebSocket features locally:

1. Start the server:
   ```cmd
   run-server.bat
   ```
   *(or `./run-server.sh`)*

2. Open your browser and navigate to:
   ```text
   http://localhost:8080
   ```

3. Open a second browser window (or incognito tab) to join as Player 2, or launch `run-client.bat` in a terminal window to play cross-platform between browser and CLI!

`Dockerfile`:

```
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

# ==============================================================================
# Stage 1: Builder
# Compiles the source code, runs all unit/integration tests, and packages the JAR.
# ==============================================================================
FROM maven:3.9.6-eclipse-temurin-21-alpine AS builder

WORKDIR /build

# Cache Maven dependencies by copying pom.xml first
COPY pom.xml .
RUN mvn dependency:go-offline -B

# Copy project source files
COPY src ./src

# Compile and package application (verifying all tests pass)
RUN mvn clean package -B

# ==============================================================================
# Stage 2: Runtime
# Ultra-lightweight and secure runtime container (~150MB).
# Runs as non-root user with Generational ZGC optimized for virtual threads.
# ==============================================================================
FROM eclipse-temurin:21-jre-alpine AS runtime

LABEL maintainer="Stanislaw" \
      description="Authoritative multiplayer Ultimo card game server" \
      version="1.0.0"

# Security best practice: run as non-privileged service user
RUN addgroup -S ultimo && adduser -S ultimo -G ultimo

WORKDIR /app

# Copy the compiled JAR artifact from the builder stage
COPY --from=builder --chown=ultimo:ultimo /build/target/ultimo-server.jar ./ultimo-server.jar

# Drop root privileges
USER ultimo

# Default TCP port for game sessions
EXPOSE 8080

# Environment variables with sensible production defaults
ENV PORT=8080 \
    JAVA_OPTS="-XX:+UseZGC -XX:+ZGenerational -Xms64m -Xmx256m"

# Periodic TCP socket healthcheck to ensure server acceptor is healthy
HEALTHCHECK --interval=15s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
    CMD nc -z 127.0.0.1 ${PORT} || exit 1

ENTRYPOINT ["sh", "-c", "exec java $JAVA_OPTS -jar ultimo-server.jar"]

```

`README.md`:

```md
# Ultimo: High-Performance Authoritative Multiplayer Game Server

[![Java Version](https://img.shields.io/badge/Java-21%20LTS-orange.svg)](https://openjdk.org/projects/jdk/21/)
[![Virtual Threads](https://img.shields.io/badge/Concurrency-Virtual%20Threads%20%28Loom%29-blue.svg)](https://openjdk.org/jeps/444)
[![Tests](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tests-81%2F81%20Passing%20%28100%25%29-brightgreen.svg)](https://junit.org/junit5/)
[![WebSockets](https://img.shields.io/badge/WebSockets-RFC%206455%20Pure%20Java-9cf.svg)](#web-client--browser-gameplay)
[![Dependencies](https://img.shields.io/badge/Runtime%20Dependencies-0%20%28Pure%20Java%20StdLib%29-success.svg)](#architectural-highlights)
[![Docker](https://img.shields.io/badge/Docker-Multi--stage%20Alpine-2496ED.svg)](https://www.docker.com/)
[![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-lightgrey.svg)](LICENSE)

An authoritative multiplayer card game server (inspired by Uno) built with **Java 21**, **Virtual Threads (Project Loom)**, **RFC 6455 WebSockets**, and **raw TCP sockets**. Built without external networking or web frameworks to maintain zero runtime dependencies, low memory footprint, and complete control over thread lifecycles and protocol framing.

---

## Architectural Highlights

- **Zero Runtime Frameworks:** Built with the standard Java 21 runtime library (`java.net`, `java.util.concurrent`, `java.io`). No external HTTP, serialization, or networking dependencies.
- **Virtual Threads (Project Loom):** Every incoming TCP client connection is handled by a lightweight Virtual Thread (`Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()`), achieving high concurrent connection scalability with minimal OS thread overhead.
- **Actor-Pattern Concurrency:** Rather than wrapping mutable game state with coarse synchronized blocks or lock contention, each active room executes a dedicated single-threaded event loop fed by a thread-safe `LinkedBlockingQueue`. This eliminates race conditions and deadlocks by design.
- **Authoritative Server with Fog of War:** Complete protection against client-side tampering or packet inspection. The server computes all moves, deck shuffles, and penalties. Opponents' cards are withheld; clients receive only their own hand and table metadata.
- **Custom Line-Delimited Protocol:** Clean, stateless, human-readable text protocol (`\n`-terminated), easily debuggable via Telnet, Netcat, or custom bots.
- **Unified Transport Multiplexer & RFC 6455 WebSockets:** A single TCP port multiplexes raw TCP socket connections, RFC 6455 WebSockets, and embedded HTML5 web assets with zero external dependencies.
- **Cloud-Native & Cross-Platform:** Complete containerization via multi-stage Alpine Dockerfile, Generational ZGC optimization (`-XX:+UseZGC -XX:+ZGenerational`), and native cross-platform runner scripts for Windows (`.bat`) and Linux/macOS (`.sh`).

---

## Design Decisions & Architecture Comparison

Comparing this implementation approach to conventional framework-based web stacks:

| Dimension | Conventional Framework Stack (e.g. Spring Boot) | Ultimo Architecture |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Networking** | High-level framework abstraction | Raw TCP sockets (`ServerSocket`, `Socket`) + in-house RFC 6455 WebSocket engine |
| **Concurrency** | Platform thread pool or reactive callbacks | Java 21 Virtual Threads (Loom) + Actor pattern event loop |
| **State Synchronization** | Coarse locks or database transactions | Single-writer Actor mailbox (`LinkedBlockingQueue`) per room |
| **State Authority** | Often delegates validation to client | Authoritative server state with Fog of War (opponent hands masked) |
| **Runtime Footprint** | 250MB+ RSS baseline memory | Lightweight footprint (<60MB RSS) with Generational ZGC |
| **Wire Protocol** | Generic JSON serialization | Line-delimited ASCII protocol with zero serialization overhead |
| **Test Suite** | Mocked controller integration tests | 80+ unit, concurrency, domain, and end-to-end integration tests |

---

## System Architecture

### 1. Component Diagram

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph Clients["Clients"]
        C1["Client 1 (CLI / Netcat)"]
        C2["Client 2 (CLI / Netcat)"]
    end

    subgraph Network["Network Layer (Virtual Threads)"]
        Acc["SocketAcceptor (Platform Thread)"]
        S1["ClientSession 1 (Virtual Thread)"]
        S2["ClientSession 2 (Virtual Thread)"]
    end

    subgraph Matchmaking["Matchmaking & Orchestration"]
        RM["RoomManager"]
    end

    subgraph Rooms["Room Layer (Actor Model)"]
        GR["GameRoom Actor Loop (Single Worker Thread)"]
        Q[("Mailbox Queue (LinkedBlockingQueue)")]
    end

    subgraph Domain["Authoritative Domain Engine"]
        Engine["UltimoEngine"]
        TM["TurnManager"]
        MV["MoveValidator"]
        Deck["Deck (108 Cards)"]
    end

    C1 -->|"Raw TCP:8080"| Acc
    C2 -->|"Raw TCP:8080"| Acc
    Acc -->|"Spawn"| S1
    Acc -->|"Spawn"| S2
    S1 -->|"Register"| RM
    S2 -->|"Register"| RM
    RM -->|"Route to Room"| GR
    S1 -->|"Enqueue Command"| Q
    S2 -->|"Enqueue Command"| Q
    Q -->|"Sequential Drain"| GR
    GR -->|"Validate & Mutate"| Engine
    Engine --> TM
    Engine --> MV
    Engine --> Deck
    GR -->|"Filtered Fog-of-War Events"| S1
    GR -->|"Filtered Fog-of-War Events"| S2
```

### 2. Actor Pattern Concurrency Model

Rather than sharing state across reader threads and struggling with lock contention or deadlocks, `GameRoom` acts as an autonomous Actor:

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    participant Client1 as Client 1 (Alice)
    participant Session1 as ClientSession 1 [V-Thread]
    participant Mailbox as Room Mailbox [LinkedBlockingQueue]
    participant Actor as Room Actor Loop [Worker Thread]
    participant Engine as UltimoEngine [Authoritative]
    participant Session2 as ClientSession 2 [V-Thread]
    participant Client2 as Client 2 (Bob)

    Client1->>Session1: TCP Wire: "PLAY RED_5\n"
    Session1->>Mailbox: offer(CommandContext(session, PLAY))
    Note over Mailbox,Actor: Commands ordered deterministically (FIFO)
    Actor->>Mailbox: take()
    Actor->>Engine: playCard("Alice", RED_5, null)
    Engine-->>Actor: Success (turn advances to Bob)
    Actor->>Session1: Send: TABLE_UPDATE RED_5 RED
    Actor->>Session1: Send: HAND [remaining cards]
    Session1-->>Client1: TCP Wire: TABLE_UPDATE ...
    Actor->>Session2: Send: TABLE_UPDATE RED_5 RED
    Actor->>Session2: Send: HAND [Bob's hand only]
    Session2-->>Client2: TCP Wire: TABLE_UPDATE ...
```

### 3. Anti-Cheat: Authoritative Fog of War

In Ultimo, the server is the absolute source of truth. Player cards remain strictly on the server:

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    ServerState["Server State (Authoritative)<br/>• Alice: [RED_7, BLUE_SKIP]<br/>• Bob: [YELLOW_3, WILD]<br/>• Draw Deck: 84 cards"]

    FOW{"Fog of War Filter"}
    ServerState --> FOW

    AliceView["Sent to Alice:<br/>• Hand: [RED_7, BLUE_SKIP]<br/>• Opponents: Bob (2 cards)"]
    BobView["Sent to Bob:<br/>• Hand: [YELLOW_3, WILD]<br/>• Opponents: Alice (2 cards)"]

    FOW --> AliceView
    FOW --> BobView
```

Neither player has any network packet or memory access to what cards the opponent holds.

---

## Wire Protocol Specification

The protocol is bidirectional, UTF-8 encoded, and line-delimited with `\n`.

### Client Commands

| Command | Arguments | Description | Example |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `JOIN` | `<username>` | Registers session identity and enters matchmaking | `JOIN Alice` |
| `START` | _(none)_ | Starts match manually when at least 2 players are in lobby | `START` |
| `PLAY` | `<card_token> [color]` | Plays a card from hand (color mandatory for wild cards) | `PLAY RED_7`<br/>`PLAY WILD_WILD BLUE` |
| `DRAW` | _(none)_ | Draws a card from the deck | `DRAW` |
| `PASS` | _(none)_ | Passes turn after drawing | `PASS` |
| `CHAT` | `<message>` | Broadcasts a chat message to room participants | `CHAT Good game!` |
| `PING` | _(none)_ | Heartbeat probe | `PING` |
| `QUIT` | _(none)_ | Gracefully leaves room and disconnects | `QUIT` |

### Server Events

| Event | Format | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `WELCOME` | `WELCOME <session_id> <message>` | Acknowledges connection and registration |
| `ROOM_STATUS`| `ROOM_STATUS <roomId> <current> <max> <p1,p2...>` | Broadcasts lobby status and connected players |
| `TOP_CARD` | `TOP_CARD <card_token> <active_color>` | Updates active discard card and table color |
| `TURN_ANNOUNCED` | `TURN_ANNOUNCED <active_player_name>` | Announces which player has the current turn |
| `HAND_UPDATE`| `HAND_UPDATE <card1> <card2> ...` | Sends player's private hand (Fog of War) |
| `OPPONENT_UPDATE`| `OPPONENT_UPDATE <opponent_name> <count>` | Broadcasts opponent's updated card count |
| `DRAW_RESULT`| `DRAW_RESULT <card_token>` | Reports card drawn to drawer |
| `CHAT_BROADCAST` | `CHAT_BROADCAST <sender> <message>` | Relays broadcast chat message or system alert |
| `GAME_OVER` | `GAME_OVER <winner_name>` | Declares match conclusion and winner name |
| `ERROR` | `ERROR <message>` | Reports syntax, domain, or turn validation error |
| `PONG` | `PONG` | Heartbeat response |

### Card Token Format

Cards are formatted as `<COLOR>_<VALUE>`:
- **Colors:** `RED`, `GREEN`, `BLUE`, `YELLOW`, `WILD`
- **Values:** `0`-`9`, `SKIP`, `REVERSE`, `DRAW_TWO`, `WILD`, `DRAW_FOUR`
- *Examples:* `RED_0`, `BLUE_SKIP`, `GREEN_DRAW_TWO`, `YELLOW_REVERSE`, `WILD_WILD`, `WILD_DRAW_FOUR`.

---

## Domain Engine & Game Rules

- **Full 108-Card Standard Deck:**
  - 19 Red, 19 Green, 19 Blue, 19 Yellow cards (one `0`, two of `1-9`).
  - 8 Skip, 8 Reverse, 8 Draw Two cards (2 of each color).
  - 4 Wild cards and 4 Wild Draw Four cards.
- **Fair Opening Discard:** If the initial revealed discard card is an action or wild card, it is returned and reshuffled until a standard number card (`0-9`) opens the table.
- **Two-Player Reverse Rule:** When only two players are in a match, playing `REVERSE` functions identically to `SKIP` (the current player immediately retains the turn).
- **Automated Discard Reshuffle:** When the draw deck is depleted, the discard pile (excluding the active top card) is automatically shuffled to replenish the deck.
- **Forfeit & Disconnect Handling:** If an opponent disconnects during an active game, remaining players win by forfeit rather than deadlocking the match loop.

---

## Web Client & Browser Gameplay

In addition to terminal clients, Ultimo ships with an embedded single-page application ([`WebClientAssets`](file:///e:/MeineProjekte/Ultimo/src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/WebClientAssets.java)) that allows anyone to play directly in a web browser without installing anything:
- **Zero-Dependency Single-Page App:** Beautiful dark-felt card table, CSS animated cards, active color indicator, turn banners, and real-time chat.
- **RFC 6455 WebSockets in Pure Java:** Custom frame encoder/decoder ([`WebSocketFrameCodec`](file:///e:/MeineProjekte/Ultimo/src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/WebSocketFrameCodec.java)) implemented without Netty, Undertow, or third-party libraries.
- **Cross-Platform Play:** CLI players and Web Browser players play together in the exact same game rooms in real time!
- **Instant Access:** Open `http://localhost:8080` in your web browser while the server is running.

---

## Terminal Client & Dual-Mode UI

The project includes an interactive terminal client ([`TerminalClientApp`](file:///e:/MeineProjekte/Ultimo/src/main/java/pl/ultimo/client/TerminalClientApp.java)) featuring:
- **Dual-Mode Rendering ([`TerminalRenderer`](file:///e:/MeineProjekte/Ultimo/src/main/java/pl/ultimo/client/TerminalRenderer.java)):** Full ANSI color cards on modern consoles (Windows Terminal, Linux, macOS) with automatic or `--plain` plaintext fallback (`[R-7]`, `[B-SKIP]`) for legacy environments.
- **Quick-Play Numeric Input:** Choose cards directly by number (`1-N`) rather than typing out token names.
- **Interactive Wild Color Picker:** Prompts for color declaration (`1:RED, 2:GREEN, 3:BLUE, 4:YELLOW`).
- **Asynchronous Wire Listener:** Real-time updates without blocking player input.

---

## Cloud Deployment & Hosting

Ultimo is designed for one-click cloud deployment. See the comprehensive [DEPLOYMENT.md](DEPLOYMENT.md) guide for details:
- **Google Cloud Run (Serverless):** Automated zero-cost hosting via `./cloudrun-deploy.sh` or `cloudrun-deploy.bat`.
- **Linux VPS (Docker Compose):** Instant single-command orchestration via `docker compose up -d`.
- **Production Reverse Proxy:** Ready-to-use Caddy and NGINX configurations with automated SSL/TLS (`wss://`).

---

## Project Directory Structure

```text
pl.ultimo
├── client
│   ├── TerminalClientApp.java       # Interactive CLI client with async event loop
│   └── TerminalRenderer.java        # ANSI & Plain-text dual-mode card renderer
├── core
│   ├── engine
│   │   ├── MoveValidator.java       # Rule validation & legal play verification
│   │   ├── TurnManager.java         # Clockwise/Counter-clockwise turn logic
│   │   └── UltimoEngine.java        # Pure domain state engine & action executor
│   ├── model
│   │   ├── Card.java                # Immutable card representation
│   │   ├── CardColor.java           # Card colors enum (RED, GREEN, BLUE, YELLOW, WILD)
│   │   └── CardValue.java           # Card faces (0-9, SKIP, REVERSE, DRAW_TWO, WILD, DRAW_FOUR)
│   └── state
│       ├── Deck.java                # 108-card deck shuffling & replenish logic
│       ├── GameState.java           # Immutable state snapshot
│       ├── Hand.java                # Player card container
│       └── Player.java              # Player domain entity
├── protocol
│   ├── ClientCommand.java           # Strongly-typed client command records
│   ├── ProtocolCodec.java           # Wire protocol serialization / deserialization
│   ├── ProtocolException.java       # Wire protocol syntax exception
│   └── ServerEvent.java             # Strongly-typed server event records
└── server
    ├── ServerApp.java               # Server bootstrap & connection orchestrator
    ├── config
    │   └── ServerConfig.java        # Server parameters & configuration record
    ├── net
    │   ├── ClientSession.java       # Universal TCP/WebSocket virtual-thread session
    │   ├── ConnectionListener.java  # Connection lifecycle event listener
    │   ├── SocketAcceptor.java      # TCP socket listener
    │   ├── WebClientAssets.java     # Embedded HTML5/CSS/JS single-page web app
    │   └── WebSocketFrameCodec.java # Pure Java RFC 6455 WebSocket frame encoder/decoder
    └── room
        ├── GameRoom.java            # Room Actor loop with LinkedBlockingQueue mailbox
        └── RoomManager.java         # Matchmaking & room lifecycle manager
```

---

## Quick Start

### Prerequisites
- **Java 21 LTS** (JDK 21 or higher)
- Optional: **Docker** & **Docker Compose**

### Option A: Web Browser (Instant Play)

1. Launch the server:
   ```cmd
   run-server.bat
   ```
   *(or `./run-server.sh` on Linux/macOS)*

2. Open your web browser and go to:
   ```text
   http://localhost:8080
   ```
3. Enter your username, click **Join Match**, and play in the browser! Open another tab to test multiplayer.

---

### Option B: Native Scripts (Zero Build Tools)

Pre-configured launch scripts are included for instant execution on any OS:

#### Windows
```cmd
:: 1. Launch the server (Window 1)
run-server.bat

:: 2. Launch Client 1 (Window 2)
run-client.bat

:: 3. Launch Client 2 (Window 3)
run-client.bat
```

#### Linux & macOS
```bash
# Make scripts executable
chmod +x run-server.sh run-client.sh

# 1. Launch the server (Terminal 1)
./run-server.sh

# 2. Launch Client 1 (Terminal 2)
./run-client.sh

# 3. Launch Client 2 (Terminal 3)
./run-client.sh
```

---

### Option C: Maven

```bash
# 1. Run all 80 tests
mvn clean test

# 2. Package standalone JAR
mvn clean package

# 3. Run server
java -jar target/ultimo-server.jar

# 4. Run terminal client
java -cp target/ultimo-server.jar pl.ultimo.client.TerminalClientApp
```

---

### Option D: Docker & Docker Compose

Launch the containerized server in one command:

```bash
# Build and run with Docker Compose
docker compose up --build
```

Or build and run the lightweight Alpine container manually:

```bash
# Build multi-stage image
docker build -t ultimo-server .

# Run on port 8080
docker run -p 8080:8080 --name ultimo-server ultimo-server
```

---

### Option E: Raw TCP Terminal (Netcat / Telnet)

Because the wire protocol is plain line-delimited text, you can connect directly with standard OS networking utilities:

```bash
nc localhost 8080
```
Then type:
```text
JOIN Alice
CHAT Hello from raw TCP!
DRAW
PASS
QUIT
```

---

## Testing & Quality Assurance

The codebase includes **80 automated unit, concurrency, domain, and end-to-end integration tests**:

- **Domain Tests:** Complete card distributions (108 cards), turn progression, reverse direction in 2-player vs. 3-player, skip logic, deck replenishment.
- **Protocol Codec Tests:** Round-trip serialization/deserialization, malformed syntax resilience, wild color validation.
- **Concurrency & Actor Tests:** Virtual thread connection handling, room mailbox FIFO ordering, client disconnect handling, room cleanup.
- **Anti-Cheat Verification:** Strict assertion that events broadcast to opponents mask private hand contents (Fog of War).
- **RFC 6455 WebSocket Tests:** Frame decoding/encoding, masked client frame parsing, official RFC 6455 SHA-1 handshake vector verification.
- **WebSocket & HTTP Integration Tests:** Full end-to-end WebSocket tests using Java 21's native `java.net.http.WebSocket`, static HTML5 client delivery, and `/health` probe verification.
- **End-to-End Game Flow:** Complete socket-level multiplayer simulation from connection and matchmaking to game completion.

```bash
# Running tests with standalone JUnit Platform launcher:
java -jar .tools/junit-platform-console-standalone-1.10.2.jar \
     execute --class-path "target/classes;target/test-classes" --scan-class-path
```

All 80 tests execute in ~2.5 seconds.

`cloudrun-deploy.bat`:

```bat
@echo off
setlocal
set SERVICE_NAME=ultimo-server
if "%GCP_REGION%"=="" set GCP_REGION=europe-west1

echo ==========================================================
echo       Deploying Ultimo Server to Google Cloud Run         
echo ==========================================================

where gcloud >nul 2>nul
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo [ERROR] Google Cloud SDK ('gcloud') is not installed or not in PATH.
    echo Install it from: https://cloud.google.com/sdk/docs/install
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo [INFO] Building and deploying '%SERVICE_NAME%' to region '%GCP_REGION%'...
call gcloud run deploy %SERVICE_NAME% ^
    --source . ^
    --region %GCP_REGION% ^
    --allow-unauthenticated ^
    --port 8080 ^
    --memory 512Mi ^
    --cpu 1 ^
    --min-instances 0 ^
    --max-instances 2 ^
    --timeout 3600 ^
    --session-affinity

if %errorlevel% equ 0 (
    echo ==========================================================
    echo [SUCCESS] Deployment completed! Your Ultimo server is live.
    echo Open the printed Service URL in your browser to play!
    echo ==========================================================
)
pause

```

`cloudrun-deploy.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e

SERVICE_NAME="${SERVICE_NAME:-ultimo-server}"
REGION="${GCP_REGION:-europe-west1}"

echo "=========================================================="
echo "      Deploying Ultimo Server to Google Cloud Run         "
echo "=========================================================="

if ! command -v gcloud &> /dev/null; then
    echo "[ERROR] Google Cloud SDK ('gcloud') is not installed or not in PATH."
    echo "Install it from: https://cloud.google.com/sdk/docs/install"
    exit 1
fi

echo "[INFO] Building and deploying '$SERVICE_NAME' to region '$REGION'..."
gcloud run deploy "$SERVICE_NAME" \
    --source . \
    --region "$REGION" \
    --allow-unauthenticated \
    --port 8080 \
    --memory 512Mi \
    --cpu 1 \
    --min-instances 0 \
    --max-instances 2 \
    --timeout 3600 \
    --session-affinity

echo "=========================================================="
echo "[SUCCESS] Deployment completed! Your Ultimo server is live."
echo "Open the Service URL in your browser to play instantly!"
echo "=========================================================="

```

`docker-compose.yml`:

```yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  ultimo-server:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    image: ultimo-server:latest
    container_name: ultimo-server
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    environment:
      - PORT=8080
      - JAVA_OPTS=-XX:+UseZGC -XX:+ZGenerational -Xms64m -Xmx256m
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "nc -z 127.0.0.1 8080 || exit 1"]
      interval: 15s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 3
      start_period: 5s
    deploy:
      resources:
        limits:
          cpus: '1.0'
          memory: 512M
        reservations:
          cpus: '0.25'
          memory: 128M

```

@echo off
title Ultimo Client

echo ===================================================

if not exist "target\classes\pl\ultimo\client\TerminalClientApp.class" (
    echo [INFO] Kompilacja projektu Java 21...
    if not exist "target\classes" mkdir "target\classes"
    javac -d target/classes src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/state/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/engine/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/protocol/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/config/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/metrics/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/room/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/*.java src/main/java/pl/ultimo/client/*.java
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo [BLAD] Blad kompilacji!
        pause
        exit /b %errorlevel%
    )
)

java -cp target/classes pl.ultimo.client.TerminalClientApp %*
pause

if [ ! -f "target/classes/pl/ultimo/client/TerminalClientApp.class" ]; then
    echo "[INFO] Kompilacja projektu Java 21..."

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/client/TerminalClientApp.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.client;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ProtocolCodec;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ServerEvent;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Interactive command-line interface client for playing Ultimo over TCP.
 * Runs a background event receiver thread while the main thread handles user input.
 */
public class TerminalClientApp {

    private final String host;
    private final int port;
    private String username;
    private final TerminalRenderer renderer;
    private final AtomicBoolean isRunning = new AtomicBoolean(true);

    private Socket socket;
    private BufferedReader reader;
    private BufferedWriter writer;

    // Client view state
    private final List<Card> myHand = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
    private final Map<String, Integer> opponentCardCounts = Collections.synchronizedMap(new LinkedHashMap<>());
    private Card topCard;
    private CardColor activeColor;
    private String activePlayerName;
    private boolean gameStarted;

    public TerminalClientApp(String host, int port, String username, boolean ansiEnabled) {
        this.host = host;
        this.port = port;
        this.username = username;
        this.renderer = new TerminalRenderer(ansiEnabled);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        String host = "localhost";
        int port = 8080;
        String name = null;
        boolean ansi = true;

        for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
            switch (args[i]) {
                case "-host" -> {
                    if (i + 1 < args.length) host = args[++i];
                }
                case "-port" -> {
                    if (i + 1 < args.length) port = Integer.parseInt(args[++i]);
                }
                case "-name" -> {
                    if (i + 1 < args.length) name = args[++i];
                }
                case "--plain", "-plain", "--no-color" -> ansi = false;
            }
        }

        Scanner scanner = new Scanner(System.in);
        if (name == null || name.isBlank()) {
            System.out.print("Podaj swój nick gracza: ");
            name = scanner.nextLine().trim();
            if (name.isEmpty()) {
                name = "Gracz_" + (System.currentTimeMillis() % 1000);
            }
        }

        TerminalClientApp client = new TerminalClientApp(host, port, name, ansi);
        client.start(scanner);
    }

    public void start(Scanner scanner) {
        System.out.println("Łączenie z serwerem Ultimo pod " + host + ":" + port + " jako '" + username + "'...");

        try {
            socket = new Socket(host, port);
            socket.setTcpNoDelay(true);
            reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(socket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(socket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

            // Start background receiver thread
            Thread.ofVirtual().name("client-receiver").start(this::receiveServerEvents);

            // Send initial JOIN command
            sendCommand("JOIN " + username);

            // Run user input loop on main thread
            runInputLoop(scanner);

        } catch (IOException e) {
            System.err.println("Nie udało się połączyć z serwerem: " + e.getMessage());
        } finally {
            disconnect();
        }
    }

    private void runInputLoop(Scanner scanner) {
        printHelp();

        while (isRunning.get() && scanner.hasNextLine()) {
            String input = scanner.nextLine().trim();
            if (input.isEmpty()) {
                continue;
            }

            if (input.equalsIgnoreCase("q") || input.equalsIgnoreCase("quit") || input.equalsIgnoreCase("exit")) {
                System.out.println("Opuszczanie gry...");
                break;
            }

            if (input.equalsIgnoreCase("h") || input.equalsIgnoreCase("help")) {
                printHelp();
                continue;
            }

            if (input.equalsIgnoreCase("t") || input.equalsIgnoreCase("table")) {
                printTableView();
                continue;
            }

            if (input.equalsIgnoreCase("s") || input.equalsIgnoreCase("start")) {
                sendCommand("START");
                continue;
            }

            if (input.equalsIgnoreCase("d") || input.equalsIgnoreCase("draw")) {
                sendCommand("DRAW");
                continue;
            }

            if (input.equalsIgnoreCase("pass") || input.equalsIgnoreCase("p")) {
                sendCommand("PASS");
                continue;
            }

            if (input.toLowerCase().startsWith("c ") || input.toLowerCase().startsWith("chat ")) {
                String msg = input.substring(input.indexOf(' ') + 1).trim();
                sendCommand("CHAT " + msg);
                continue;
            }

            // Single or multi-card combo play by card numbers [e.g. "1", "1,2", "1 2", "play 1,2"]
            String playTarget = input;
            if (playTarget.toLowerCase().startsWith("play ")) {
                playTarget = playTarget.substring(5).trim();
            }

            if (playTarget.matches("^[0-9]+([\\s,]+[0-9]+)*$")) {
                String[] tokens = playTarget.split("[\\s,]+");
                List<Integer> selectedIndices = new ArrayList<>();
                Set<Integer> uniqueCheck = new HashSet<>();
                boolean validIndices = true;

                synchronized (myHand) {
                    for (String token : tokens) {
                        try {
                            int index = Integer.parseInt(token) - 1;
                            if (index < 0 || index >= myHand.size()) {
                                System.out.println("Niepoprawny numer karty: " + token + "! Dostępne numery: 1 do " + myHand.size());
                                validIndices = false;
                                break;
                            }
                            if (!uniqueCheck.add(index)) {
                                System.out.println("Karta #" + token + " została podana więcej niż raz! Każda karta z ręki ma swój unikalny numer.");
                                validIndices = false;
                                break;
                            }
                            selectedIndices.add(index);
                        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                            validIndices = false;
                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    if (!validIndices || selectedIndices.isEmpty()) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    List<Card> cardsToPlay = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (int idx : selectedIndices) {
                        cardsToPlay.add(myHand.get(idx));
                    }

                    boolean hasWild = cardsToPlay.stream().anyMatch(Card::isWild);
                    String cardsStr = cardsToPlay.stream()
                            .map(ProtocolCodec::formatCard)
                            .collect(Collectors.joining(","));

                    if (hasWild) {
                        CardColor chosenColor = promptWildColor(scanner);
                        sendCommand("PLAY " + cardsStr + " " + chosenColor.name());
                    } else {
                        sendCommand("PLAY " + cardsStr);
                    }
                }
                continue;
            }

            // Raw protocol command fallback (e.g. "PLAY RED_5")
            sendCommand(input);
        }
    }

    private CardColor promptWildColor(Scanner scanner) {
        while (true) {
            System.out.print("Wybierz kolor dla dzikiej karty [R=Czerwony, Y=Żółty, G=Zielony, B=Niebieski]: ");
            String choice = scanner.nextLine().trim().toUpperCase();
            switch (choice) {
                case "R", "RED", "CZERWONY" -> { return CardColor.RED; }
                case "Y", "YELLOW", "ZOLTY", "ŻÓŁTY" -> { return CardColor.YELLOW; }
                case "G", "GREEN", "ZIELONY" -> { return CardColor.GREEN; }
                case "B", "BLUE", "NIEBIESKI" -> { return CardColor.BLUE; }
                default -> System.out.println("Niepoprawny wybór. Wpisz R, Y, G lub B.");
            }
        }
    }

    private void receiveServerEvents() {
        try {
            String line;
            while (isRunning.get() && (line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                if (line.isBlank()) {
                    continue;
                }
                try {
                    ServerEvent event = ProtocolCodec.decodeEvent(line);
                    handleEvent(event);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    System.out.println("[Serwer]: " + line);
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            if (isRunning.get()) {
                System.out.println("\nPołączenie z serwerem zostało zamknięte.");
            }
        } finally {
            isRunning.set(false);
        }
    }

    private void handleEvent(ServerEvent event) {
        switch (event) {
            case ServerEvent.Welcome welcome -> {
                System.out.println("\n*** " + welcome.message() + " ***");
                System.out.println("Twój identyfikator gracza: " + welcome.assignedPlayerId());
            }
            case ServerEvent.RoomStatus roomStatus -> {
                System.out.println("\n[LOBBY] Pokój " + roomStatus.roomId() + ": Gracze ("
                        + roomStatus.currentPlayers() + "/" + roomStatus.maxPlayers() + "): "
                        + String.join(", ", roomStatus.playerNames()));
                if (roomStatus.currentPlayers() < roomStatus.maxPlayers()) {
                    if (roomStatus.currentPlayers() >= 2) {
                        System.out.println("Możesz wpisać 'start' (lub 's'), aby rozpocząć grę w " + roomStatus.currentPlayers() + " graczy, lub poczekać na kolejnych graczy.");
                    } else {
                        System.out.println("Oczekiwanie na dołączenie drugiego gracza...");
                    }
                }
            }
            case ServerEvent.TopCard top -> {
                this.topCard = top.card();
                this.activeColor = top.activeColor();
                this.gameStarted = true;
                printTableView();
            }
            case ServerEvent.HandUpdate handUpdate -> {
                synchronized (myHand) {
                    myHand.clear();
                    myHand.addAll(handUpdate.cards());
                }
                if (gameStarted) {
                    printTableView();
                }
            }
            case ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced turn -> {
                this.activePlayerName = turn.activePlayerName();
                if (gameStarted) {
                    printTableView();
                }
            }
            case ServerEvent.OpponentUpdate opp -> {
                opponentCardCounts.put(opp.opponentName(), opp.cardCount());
            }
            case ServerEvent.DrawResult draw -> {
                System.out.println("\n[+] Dobrałeś kartę: " + renderer.renderCard(draw.drawnCard()));
            }
            case ServerEvent.ErrorEvent err -> {
                System.out.println("\n[-] BŁĄD RUCHU: " + err.errorMessage());
            }
            case ServerEvent.ChatBroadcast chat -> {
                System.out.println("\n[CZAT] " + chat.sender() + ": " + chat.message());
            }
            case ServerEvent.GameOver gameOver -> {
                System.out.println("\n============================================================");
                System.out.println("               *** KONIEC MECZU ULTIMO ***                  ");
                System.out.println("  Zwycięzca: " + gameOver.winnerName());
                System.out.println("============================================================\n");
            }
            case ServerEvent.ServerShutdown shutdown -> {
                System.out.println("\n[!] OSTRZEŻENIE: Serwer jest wyłączany za " + shutdown.drainSeconds() + " sekund (graceful shutdown)...");
                isRunning.set(false);
            }
            case ServerEvent.Pong ignored -> {}
        }
    }

    private synchronized void printTableView() {
        if (!gameStarted) {
            return;
        }

        System.out.print("\n" + renderer.renderTable(topCard, activeColor, activePlayerName, username, opponentCardCounts));
        System.out.println("Twoje karty:");
        synchronized (myHand) {
            System.out.println(renderer.renderHand(myHand));
        }
        System.out.print("Wybór [1-" + myHand.size() + " / kombo np. 1,2 / d / pass / help]> ");
    }

    private void sendCommand(String commandLine) {
        try {
            writer.write(commandLine.endsWith("\n") ? commandLine : commandLine + "\n");
            writer.flush();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            System.err.println("Błąd wysyłania komendy: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void printHelp() {
        System.out.println("\n--- DOSTĘPNE POLECENIA ---");
        System.out.println("  [1, 2... / 1,2 / 1 2] - Zagranie karty lub komba kart o tej samej wartości (np. 1,3)");
        System.out.println("  s, start              - Rozpoczęcie meczu (gdy w pokoju jest min. 2 graczy)");
        System.out.println("  d, draw               - Dobranie 1 karty z talii");
        System.out.println("  p, pass               - Spasowanie tury (dozwolone po dobraniu karty)");
        System.out.println("  c <treść>             - Wysłanie wiadomości na czacie pokojowym");
        System.out.println("  t, table              - Ponowne wyświetlenie stołu i kart");
        System.out.println("  h, help               - Wyświetlenie tej pomocy");
        System.out.println("  q, quit               - Wyjście z gry");
        System.out.println("--------------------------\n");
    }

    public void disconnect() {
        isRunning.set(false);
        try {
            if (socket != null && !socket.isClosed()) {
                socket.close();
            }
        } catch (IOException ignored) {}
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/client/TerminalRenderer.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.client;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Renders Ultimo cards, hands, and game table in the terminal.
 * Supports dual-mode: ANSI 16-color rich rendering and plain-text fallback.
 */
public class TerminalRenderer {

    // ANSI Color Codes
    private static final String RESET = "\u001B[0m";
    private static final String BOLD = "\u001B[1m";

    private static final String BG_RED = "\u001B[41m\u001B[97m";       // White text on Red
    private static final String BG_GREEN = "\u001B[42m\u001B[97m";     // White text on Green
    private static final String BG_BLUE = "\u001B[44m\u001B[97m";      // White text on Blue
    private static final String BG_YELLOW = "\u001B[43m\u001B[30m";    // Black text on Yellow
    private static final String BG_WILD = "\u001B[45m\u001B[97m";      // White text on Magenta

    private static final String FG_RED = "\u001B[91m";
    private static final String FG_GREEN = "\u001B[92m";
    private static final String FG_BLUE = "\u001B[94m";
    private static final String FG_YELLOW = "\u001B[93m";
    private static final String FG_WILD = "\u001B[95m";

    private final boolean ansiEnabled;

    public TerminalRenderer(boolean ansiEnabled) {
        this.ansiEnabled = ansiEnabled;
    }

    public static TerminalRenderer colored() {
        return new TerminalRenderer(true);
    }

    public static TerminalRenderer plain() {
        return new TerminalRenderer(false);
    }

    /**
     * Formats a single card for terminal display.
     */
    public String renderCard(Card card) {
        if (card == null) {
            return "[ BRAK ]";
        }

        String label = switch (card.value()) {
            case ZERO -> "0";
            case ONE -> "1";
            case TWO -> "2";
            case THREE -> "3";
            case FOUR -> "4";
            case FIVE -> "5";
            case SIX -> "6";
            case SEVEN -> "7";
            case EIGHT -> "8";
            case NINE -> "9";
            case SKIP -> "SKIP";
            case REVERSE -> "REV";
            case DRAW_TWO -> "+2";
            case WILD -> "WILD";
            case WILD_DRAW_FOUR -> "WILD+4";
        };

        if (!ansiEnabled) {
            return "[" + card.color() + " " + label + "]";
        }

        String bg = switch (card.color()) {
            case RED -> BG_RED;
            case GREEN -> BG_GREEN;
            case BLUE -> BG_BLUE;
            case YELLOW -> BG_YELLOW;
            case WILD -> BG_WILD;
        };

        return bg + BOLD + " " + card.color() + " " + label + " " + RESET;
    }

    /**
     * Formats the active color banner.
     */
    public String renderColorBanner(CardColor color) {
        if (color == null) {
            return "-";
        }
        if (!ansiEnabled) {
            return color.name();
        }

        String fg = switch (color) {
            case RED -> FG_RED;
            case GREEN -> FG_GREEN;
            case BLUE -> FG_BLUE;
            case YELLOW -> FG_YELLOW;
            case WILD -> FG_WILD;
        };

        return fg + BOLD + color.name() + RESET;
    }

    /**
     * Formats the player's hand with 1-based indices for quick play selection.
     */
    public String renderHand(List<Card> cards) {
        if (cards == null || cards.isEmpty()) {
            return "Twoja ręka jest pusta.";
        }

        String bold = ansiEnabled ? BOLD : "";
        String reset = ansiEnabled ? RESET : "";

        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < cards.size(); i++) {
            sb.append(bold).append("[").append(i + 1).append("] ").append(reset);
            sb.append(renderCard(cards.get(i)));
            sb.append("   ");
            if ((i + 1) % 4 == 0 && i < cards.size() - 1) {
                sb.append("\n");
            }
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    /**
     * Formats the game table view: top card, active color, opponents, and turn status.
     */
    public String renderTable(
            Card topCard,
            CardColor activeColor,
            String activePlayer,
            String myUsername,
            Map<String, Integer> opponentCardCounts) {

        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("============================================================\n");
        sb.append("  STÓŁ ULTIMO\n");
        sb.append("------------------------------------------------------------\n");

        sb.append("  Karta na wierzchu: ").append(renderCard(topCard)).append("\n");
        sb.append("  Aktywny kolor:     ").append(renderColorBanner(activeColor)).append("\n");

        if (opponentCardCounts != null && !opponentCardCounts.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append("  Przeciwnicy:       ");
            opponentCardCounts.forEach((name, count) -> {
                if (!name.equalsIgnoreCase(myUsername)) {
                    sb.append(name).append(" (").append(count).append(" kart)  ");
                }
            });
            sb.append("\n");
        }

        sb.append("------------------------------------------------------------\n");
        boolean isMyTurn = myUsername != null && myUsername.equalsIgnoreCase(activePlayer);
        if (isMyTurn) {
            if (ansiEnabled) {
                sb.append("  ").append(FG_GREEN).append(BOLD).append(">>> TWOJA TURA! Wybierz numer karty [1-N] lub wpisz 'd' (dociągnij) <<<").append(RESET).append("\n");
            } else {
                sb.append("  >>> TWOJA TURA! Wybierz numer karty [1-N] lub wpisz 'd' (dociągnij) <<<\n");
            }
        } else {
            sb.append("  Aktualna tura:     ").append(activePlayer != null ? activePlayer : "-").append(" (czekaj na swój ruch)\n");
        }
        sb.append("============================================================\n");

        return sb.toString();
    }

    public boolean isAnsiEnabled() {
        return ansiEnabled;
    }
}

package pl.ultimo.core.engine;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Player;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Immutable report representing the final outcome of an Ultimo game.
 *
 * @param winner the winning player
 * @param finalScores map of player username to their accumulated round points
 */
public record GameResult(
        Player winner,
        Map<String, Integer> finalScores
) {
    public int getWinnerScore() {
        return (finalScores != null && winner != null)
                ? finalScores.getOrDefault(winner.getUsername(), 0)
                : 0;
    }
}

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/engine/MoveValidator.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.engine;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardValue;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Player;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.PlayerHand;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Pure domain validator enforcing official Ultimo move legality rules.
 */
public final class MoveValidator {

    private MoveValidator() {
        // Utility class
    }

    /**
     * Checks if a card is legally playable against the active table state.
     *
     * @param candidateCard the card the player intends to play
     * @param topCard the top card on the discard pile
     * @param activeColor the required color (either topCard's color or chosen wild color)
     * @return true if the play is legally valid
     */
    public static boolean isValidPlay(Card candidateCard, Card topCard, CardColor activeColor) {
        if (candidateCard == null || topCard == null || activeColor == null) {
            return false;
        }

        if (candidateCard.isWild()) {
            return true;
        }

        return candidateCard.color() == activeColor || candidateCard.value() == topCard.value();
    }

    /**
     * Comprehensive validation of an attempted move by a player.
     *
     * @return empty Optional if valid, or an Optional containing a descriptive error reason
     */
    public static Optional<String> validateMove(
            Player player,
            Player activePlayer,
            Card card,
            Card topCard,
            CardColor activeColor,
            CardColor chosenWildColor) {

        if (!player.equals(activePlayer)) {
            return Optional.of("It is not your turn");
        }

        if (!player.getHand().contains(card)) {
            return Optional.of("You do not hold card: " + card);
        }

        if (card.isWild()) {
            if (chosenWildColor == null || !chosenWildColor.isStandardColor()) {
                return Optional.of("Must select a valid color (RED, YELLOW, GREEN, BLUE) for wild card");
            }
        } else if (!isValidPlay(card, topCard, activeColor)) {
            return Optional.of("Card " + card + " cannot be played on " + topCard + " (active color: " + activeColor + ")");
        }

        return Optional.empty();
    }

    /**
     * Comprehensive validation of an attempted multi-card combo move by a player.
     * All cards must share the same face value, the player must hold all cards in hand,
     * and at least one card in the combo must match the current table state.
     *
     * @return empty Optional if valid, or an Optional containing a descriptive error reason
     */
    public static Optional<String> validateMultiMove(
            Player player,
            Player activePlayer,
            List<Card> cards,
            Card topCard,
            CardColor activeColor,
            CardColor chosenWildColor) {

        if (!player.equals(activePlayer)) {
            return Optional.of("It is not your turn");
        }

        if (cards == null || cards.isEmpty()) {
            return Optional.of("No cards specified to play");
        }

        if (cards.size() == 1) {
            return validateMove(player, activePlayer, cards.get(0), topCard, activeColor, chosenWildColor);
        }

        // Verify player holds all specified cards (accounting for duplicates)
        Map<Card, Long> requiredCounts = cards.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Function.identity(), Collectors.counting()));
        Map<Card, Long> handCounts = player.getHand().getCards().stream()
                .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(Function.identity(), Collectors.counting()));

        for (Map.Entry<Card, Long> entry : requiredCounts.entrySet()) {
            long inHand = handCounts.getOrDefault(entry.getKey(), 0L);
            if (inHand < entry.getValue()) {
                return Optional.of("You do not hold card: " + entry.getKey());
            }
        }

        // All cards in a combo must share the same card value
        CardValue firstValue = cards.get(0).value();
        boolean allSameValue = cards.stream().allMatch(c -> c.value() == firstValue);
        if (!allSameValue) {
            return Optional.of("All cards in a combo must share the same face value");
        }

        // At least one card in the combo must match the current table
        boolean canMatchTable = cards.stream().anyMatch(c -> isValidPlay(c, topCard, activeColor));
        if (!canMatchTable) {
            return Optional.of("None of the cards in the combo (" + cards + ") can be played on " + topCard + " (active color: " + activeColor + ")");
        }

        // Validate wild color declaration if any card in the combo is wild
        boolean hasWild = cards.stream().anyMatch(Card::isWild);
        if (hasWild) {
            if (chosenWildColor == null || !chosenWildColor.isStandardColor()) {
                return Optional.of("Must select a valid color (RED, YELLOW, GREEN, BLUE) for wild card");
            }
        }

        return Optional.empty();
    }

    /**
     * Checks if a player has any legally playable cards in hand.
     */
    public static boolean hasPlayableCard(PlayerHand hand, Card topCard, CardColor activeColor) {
        if (hand == null || topCard == null || activeColor == null) {
            return false;
        }
        return hand.getCards().stream()
                .anyMatch(card -> isValidPlay(card, topCard, activeColor));
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/engine/UltimoEngine.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.engine;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Deck;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.DiscardPile;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Player;
import pl.ultimo.core.state.GameState;
import pl.ultimo.core.state.TurnManager;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Random;

/**
 * Authoritative game engine orchestrating Ultimo rules, turn progression, and card resolution.
 * Completely decoupled from networking.
 */
public class UltimoEngine {

    public static final int STARTING_HAND_SIZE = 7;

    private final List<Player> players;
    private final Deck deck;
    private final DiscardPile discardPile;
    private final TurnManager turnManager;
    private final Random random;

    private boolean isGameStarted;
    private boolean isGameOver;
    private boolean hasDrawnThisTurn;
    private Player winner;

    public record PlayResult(
            boolean success,
            String errorMessage,
            List<Card> playedCards,
            boolean isUltimo,
            boolean isGameOver,
            Player winner
    ) {
        public Card playedCard() {
            return playedCards != null && !playedCards.isEmpty()
                    ? playedCards.get(playedCards.size() - 1)
                    : null;
        }

        public static PlayResult failure(String errorMessage) {
            return new PlayResult(false, errorMessage, List.of(), false, false, null);
        }

        public static PlayResult success(List<Card> cards, boolean isUltimo, boolean isGameOver, Player winner) {
            return new PlayResult(true, null, cards, isUltimo, isGameOver, winner);
        }

        public static PlayResult success(Card card, boolean isUltimo, boolean isGameOver, Player winner) {
            return new PlayResult(true, null, List.of(card), isUltimo, isGameOver, winner);
        }
    }

    public UltimoEngine(List<Player> players) {
        this(players, new Random());
    }

    public UltimoEngine(List<Player> players, Random random) {
        if (players == null || players.size() < 2) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("At least 2 players are required to start Ultimo");
        }
        this.players = List.copyOf(players);
        this.random = Objects.requireNonNull(random, "random must not be null");
        this.deck = Deck.createStandardDeck(random);
        this.discardPile = new DiscardPile();
        this.turnManager = new TurnManager(this.players);
    }

    /**
     * Initializes the match: deals starting hands and flips the first card onto the discard pile.
     */
    public void start() {
        if (isGameStarted) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Game has already started");
        }

        // Deal 7 cards to each player
        for (int i = 0; i < STARTING_HAND_SIZE; i++) {
            for (Player player : players) {
                drawCardFromDeck().ifPresent(player.getHand()::addCard);
            }
        }

        // Flip starting card; if action card or wild is drawn as first card, return to deck
        // ensuring fair start where every player starts with 7 cards and player 0 plays first
        Card startingCard;
        do {
            startingCard = drawCardFromDeck().orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("Deck ran out of cards during setup"));
            if (!startingCard.value().isNumber()) {
                deck.replenishFromDiscard(List.of(startingCard));
                startingCard = null;
            }
        } while (startingCard == null);

        discardPile.playCard(startingCard, startingCard.color());

        this.isGameStarted = true;
        this.hasDrawnThisTurn = false;
    }

    /**
     * Attempts to play a single card from the active player's hand.
     */
    public PlayResult playCard(Player player, Card card, CardColor chosenWildColor) {
        return playCards(player, List.of(card), chosenWildColor);
    }

    /**
     * Attempts to play one or more cards (multi-card combo) from the active player's hand.
     */
    public PlayResult playCards(Player player, List<Card> cards, CardColor chosenWildColor) {
        if (!isGameStarted) {
            return PlayResult.failure("Game has not started yet");
        }
        if (isGameOver) {
            return PlayResult.failure("Game is already over");
        }
        if (cards == null || cards.isEmpty()) {
            return PlayResult.failure("No cards specified to play");
        }

        Card topCard = discardPile.getTopCard().orElse(null);
        CardColor activeColor = discardPile.getActiveColor();

        Optional<String> validationError = MoveValidator.validateMultiMove(
                player,
                turnManager.getCurrentPlayer(),
                cards,
                topCard,
                activeColor,
                chosenWildColor
        );

        if (validationError.isPresent()) {
            return PlayResult.failure(validationError.get());
        }

        // Determine card play order: a card that matches table must be placed first as initiator
        List<Card> orderedCards = new ArrayList<>(cards);
        if (!MoveValidator.isValidPlay(orderedCards.get(0), topCard, activeColor)) {
            for (int i = 1; i < orderedCards.size(); i++) {
                if (MoveValidator.isValidPlay(orderedCards.get(i), topCard, activeColor)) {
                    Card initiator = orderedCards.remove(i);
                    orderedCards.add(0, initiator);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        // Remove cards from player hand and place onto discard pile in order
        for (Card card : orderedCards) {
            player.getHand().removeCard(card);
            CardColor effectiveColor = card.isWild() ? chosenWildColor : card.color();
            discardPile.playCard(card, effectiveColor);
        }

        // Check if player has won
        if (player.getHand().isEmpty()) {
            this.isGameOver = true;
            this.winner = player;
            return PlayResult.success(orderedCards, false, true, player);
        }

        boolean isUltimo = player.getHand().size() == 1;

        // Apply combo action effects
        applyComboAction(orderedCards);

        this.hasDrawnThisTurn = false;
        return PlayResult.success(orderedCards, isUltimo, false, null);
    }

    /**
     * Draws one card from the draw pile for the active player.
     * Can only be performed once per turn.
     */
    public Optional<Card> drawCard(Player player) {
        if (!isGameStarted || isGameOver) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        if (!player.equals(turnManager.getCurrentPlayer())) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        if (hasDrawnThisTurn) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        Optional<Card> drawn = drawCardFromDeck();
        drawn.ifPresent(card -> {
            player.getHand().addCard(card);
            this.hasDrawnThisTurn = true;
        });

        return drawn;
    }

    /**
     * Passes the turn after drawing a card.
     */
    public boolean passTurn(Player player) {
        if (!isGameStarted || isGameOver) {
            return false;
        }
        if (!player.equals(turnManager.getCurrentPlayer())) {
            return false;
        }
        if (!hasDrawnThisTurn) {
            return false; // In Ultimo, player may only pass after drawing
        }

        turnManager.advanceTurn();
        this.hasDrawnThisTurn = false;
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Creates an immutable snapshot of current game state for serialization and broadcasting.
     */
    public GameState captureSnapshot() {
        Map<String, Integer> cardCounts = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (Player p : players) {
            cardCounts.put(p.getUsername(), p.getHand().size());
        }

        return new GameState(
                turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername(),
                discardPile.getTopCard().orElse(null),
                discardPile.getActiveColor(),
                turnManager.getDirection(),
                Collections.unmodifiableMap(cardCounts),
                deck.size(),
                isGameOver,
                winner != null ? winner.getUsername() : null
        );
    }

    /**
     * Calculates final round scores. The winner receives points corresponding
     * to the sum of cards remaining in all opponents' hands.
     */
    public Optional<GameResult> getResult() {
        if (!isGameOver || winner == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        int totalWinnerPoints = 0;
        Map<String, Integer> scores = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        for (Player p : players) {
            if (!p.equals(winner)) {
                totalWinnerPoints += p.getHand().calculateTotalScore();
                scores.put(p.getUsername(), 0);
            }
        }
        scores.put(winner.getUsername(), totalWinnerPoints);

        return Optional.of(new GameResult(winner, Collections.unmodifiableMap(scores)));
    }

    private void applyComboAction(List<Card> cards) {
        int count = cards.size();
        Card lastCard = cards.get(cards.size() - 1);
        switch (lastCard.value()) {
            case SKIP -> turnManager.advanceTurn(1 + count);
            case REVERSE -> {
                if (turnManager.isTwoPlayerGame()) {
                    if (count % 2 == 1) {
                        turnManager.advanceTurn(2); // In 2-player Ultimo, odd number of reverses acts as Skip
                    } else {
                        turnManager.advanceTurn(1); // Even number of reverses cancel out
                    }
                } else {
                    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                        turnManager.reverseDirection();
                    }
                    turnManager.advanceTurn(1);
                }
            }
            case DRAW_TWO -> {
                Player victim = turnManager.peekNextPlayer();
                drawCardsFor(victim, 2 * count);
                turnManager.skipTurn();
            }
            case WILD_DRAW_FOUR -> {
                Player victim = turnManager.peekNextPlayer();
                drawCardsFor(victim, 4 * count);
                turnManager.skipTurn();
            }
            case WILD, ZERO, ONE, TWO, THREE, FOUR, FIVE, SIX, SEVEN, EIGHT, NINE -> turnManager.advanceTurn();
        }
    }

    private void drawCardsFor(Player target, int count) {
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            drawCardFromDeck().ifPresent(target.getHand()::addCard);
        }
    }

    private Optional<Card> drawCardFromDeck() {
        if (deck.isEmpty()) {
            List<Card> recycled = discardPile.extractCardsForReplenish();
            deck.replenishFromDiscard(recycled);
        }
        return deck.draw();
    }

    public List<Player> getPlayers() {
        return players;
    }

    public TurnManager getTurnManager() {
        return turnManager;
    }

    public DiscardPile getDiscardPile() {
        return discardPile;
    }

    public Deck getDeck() {
        return deck;
    }

    public boolean isGameStarted() {
        return isGameStarted;
    }

    public boolean isGameOver() {
        return isGameOver;
    }

    public Player getWinner() {
        return winner;
    }

    public boolean hasDrawnThisTurn() {
        return hasDrawnThisTurn;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/Card.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.model;

import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Immutable representation of a single Ultimo card.
 *
 * @param color the color of the card
 * @param value the face value or action type of the card
 */
public record Card(CardColor color, CardValue value) {

    public Card {
        Objects.requireNonNull(color, "color must not be null");
        Objects.requireNonNull(value, "value must not be null");
        if (value.isWild() && color != CardColor.WILD) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Wild cards must have WILD color");
        }
        if (!value.isWild() && color == CardColor.WILD) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Standard cards cannot have WILD color");
        }
    }

    public boolean isWild() {
        return value.isWild();
    }

    public boolean isAction() {
        return value.isAction();
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return color + "_" + value;
    }
}

```

package pl.ultimo.core.model;

/**
 * Represents the four standard Ultimo colors plus a special WILD color for wild cards.
 */
public enum CardColor {
    RED,
    YELLOW,
    GREEN,
    BLUE,
    WILD;

    /**
     * Checks if this color is a playable suit (i.e. not WILD).
     */
    public boolean isStandardColor() {
        return this != WILD;
    }
}

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/CardValue.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.model;

/**
 * Represents the face values and special action types of an Ultimo card.
 */
public enum CardValue {
    ZERO(0),
    ONE(1),
    TWO(2),
    THREE(3),
    FOUR(4),
    FIVE(5),
    SIX(6),
    SEVEN(7),
    EIGHT(8),
    NINE(9),
    SKIP(20),
    REVERSE(20),
    DRAW_TWO(20),
    WILD(50),
    WILD_DRAW_FOUR(50);

    private final int pointValue;

    CardValue(int pointValue) {
        this.pointValue = pointValue;
    }

    public int getPointValue() {
        return pointValue;
    }

    public boolean isNumber() {
        return ordinal() <= NINE.ordinal();
    }

    public boolean isAction() {
        return this == SKIP || this == REVERSE || this == DRAW_TWO;
    }

    public boolean isWild() {
        return this == WILD || this == WILD_DRAW_FOUR;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/Deck.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.model;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Random;

/**
 * Represents the draw deck containing Ultimo cards.
 * Standard deck consists of exactly 108 cards:
 * - 19 Blue cards (one 0, two of 1-9)
 * - 19 Green cards (one 0, two of 1-9)
 * - 19 Red cards (one 0, two of 1-9)
 * - 19 Yellow cards (one 0, two of 1-9)
 * - 8 Draw Two cards (two in each color)
 * - 8 Reverse cards (two in each color)
 * - 8 Skip cards (two in each color)
 * - 4 Wild cards
 * - 4 Wild Draw Four cards
 */
public class Deck {

    private final Deque<Card> cards;
    private final Random random;

    public Deck() {
        this(new Random());
    }

    public Deck(Random random) {
        this.cards = new ArrayDeque<>();
        this.random = random;
    }

    /**
     * Creates and shuffles a standard 108-card Ultimo deck.
     */
    public static Deck createStandardDeck() {
        return createStandardDeck(new Random());
    }

    public static Deck createStandardDeck(Random random) {
        Deck deck = new Deck(random);
        List<Card> initialCards = new ArrayList<>(108);

        for (CardColor color : CardColor.values()) {
            if (!color.isStandardColor()) {
                continue;
            }

            // Exactly one 0 per color
            initialCards.add(new Card(color, CardValue.ZERO));

            // Two of each 1-9 per color
            for (int number = 1; number <= 9; number++) {
                CardValue val = CardValue.valueOf(getNumberName(number));
                initialCards.add(new Card(color, val));
                initialCards.add(new Card(color, val));
            }

            // Two of each action card per color
            initialCards.add(new Card(color, CardValue.SKIP));
            initialCards.add(new Card(color, CardValue.SKIP));
            initialCards.add(new Card(color, CardValue.REVERSE));
            initialCards.add(new Card(color, CardValue.REVERSE));
            initialCards.add(new Card(color, CardValue.DRAW_TWO));
            initialCards.add(new Card(color, CardValue.DRAW_TWO));
        }

        // Four Wild cards and four Wild Draw Four cards
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            initialCards.add(new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD));
            initialCards.add(new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD_DRAW_FOUR));
        }

        Collections.shuffle(initialCards, random);
        deck.cards.addAll(initialCards);
        return deck;
    }

    public void shuffle() {
        List<Card> cardList = new ArrayList<>(cards);
        Collections.shuffle(cardList, random);
        cards.clear();
        cards.addAll(cardList);
    }

    public Optional<Card> draw() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(cards.pollFirst());
    }

    public void replenishFromDiscard(List<Card> recycledCards) {
        if (recycledCards == null || recycledCards.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        List<Card> toShuffle = new ArrayList<>(recycledCards);
        Collections.shuffle(toShuffle, random);
        cards.addAll(toShuffle);
    }

    public int size() {
        return cards.size();
    }

    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return cards.isEmpty();
    }

    private static String getNumberName(int number) {
        return switch (number) {
            case 1 -> "ONE";
            case 2 -> "TWO";
            case 3 -> "THREE";
            case 4 -> "FOUR";
            case 5 -> "FIVE";
            case 6 -> "SIX";
            case 7 -> "SEVEN";
            case 8 -> "EIGHT";
            case 9 -> "NINE";
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid number: " + number);
        };
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/DiscardPile.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.model;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Manages cards that have been played onto the table.
 * Tracks the current active card and the declared color (for wild cards).
 */
public class DiscardPile {

    private final Deque<Card> playedCards = new ArrayDeque<>();
    private CardColor activeColor;

    public void playCard(Card card, CardColor chosenColor) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(card, "card must not be null");
        if (card.isWild()) {
            if (chosenColor == null || !chosenColor.isStandardColor()) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Must declare a valid standard color when playing a wild card");
            }
            this.activeColor = chosenColor;
        } else {
            this.activeColor = card.color();
        }
        playedCards.addFirst(card);
    }

    public Optional<Card> getTopCard() {
        return Optional.ofNullable(playedCards.peekFirst());
    }

    public CardColor getActiveColor() {
        return activeColor;
    }

    /**
     * Retains the current top card on the pile and removes all remaining
     * cards so they can be recycled back into the draw deck.
     */
    public List<Card> extractCardsForReplenish() {
        if (playedCards.size() <= 1) {
            return List.of();
        }

        Card topCard = playedCards.pollFirst();
        List<Card> recycledCards = new ArrayList<>(playedCards);
        playedCards.clear();
        playedCards.addFirst(topCard);

        return recycledCards;
    }

    public int size() {
        return playedCards.size();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/Player.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.model;

import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Domain entity representing a participant in an Ultimo game session.
 */
public class Player {

    private final String id;
    private final String username;
    private final PlayerHand hand;

    public Player(String id, String username) {
        this.id = Objects.requireNonNull(id, "id must not be null");
        this.username = Objects.requireNonNull(username, "username must not be null");
        this.hand = new PlayerHand();
    }

    public String getId() {
        return id;
    }

    public String getUsername() {
        return username;
    }

    public PlayerHand getHand() {
        return hand;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (!(o instanceof Player player)) return false;
        return id.equals(player.id);
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return id.hashCode();
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return username + " (id=" + id + ")";
    }
}

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/PlayerHand.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.model;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Encapsulates the collection of cards held by a player.
 */
public class PlayerHand {

    private final List<Card> cards = new ArrayList<>();

    public void addCard(Card card) {
        cards.add(Objects.requireNonNull(card, "card must not be null"));
    }

    public boolean removeCard(Card card) {
        return cards.remove(card);
    }

    public boolean contains(Card card) {
        return cards.contains(card);
    }

    public List<Card> getCards() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(cards);
    }

    /**
     * Calculates the sum of points of all cards in hand using official Ultimo point values.
     */
    public int calculateTotalScore() {
        return cards.stream()
                .mapToInt(card -> card.value().getPointValue())
                .sum();
    }

    public int size() {
        return cards.size();
    }

    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return cards.isEmpty();
    }

    public void clear() {
        cards.clear();
    }
}

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/state/Direction.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.state;

/**
 * Defines the play rotation direction in an Ultimo match.
 */
public enum Direction {
    CLOCKWISE(1),
    COUNTER_CLOCKWISE(-1);

    private final int step;

    Direction(int step) {
        this.step = step;
    }

    public int getStep() {
        return step;
    }

    public Direction toggle() {
        return this == CLOCKWISE ? COUNTER_CLOCKWISE : CLOCKWISE;
    }
}
