```java
package pl.ultimo.core.state;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Immutable snapshot of the game state suitable for broadcasting or inspection.
 *
 * @param activePlayerName username of the player whose turn it currently is
 * @param topCard top card currently visible on the discard pile
 * @param activeColor effective color required for the next play (e.g. declared wild color)
 * @param direction current direction of play
 * @param opponentCardCounts map of player username to number of cards held
 * @param deckCardCount remaining cards in draw deck
 * @param isGameOver whether the match has completed
 * @param winnerName username of the winner, if game is over
 */
public record GameState(
        String activePlayerName,
        Card topCard,
        CardColor activeColor,
        Direction direction,
        Map<String, Integer> opponentCardCounts,
        int deckCardCount,
        boolean isGameOver,
        String winnerName
) {}
```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/core/state/TurnManager.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.state;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Player;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Manages cyclic player rotation, skips, and direction reversals according to official Ultimo rules.
 */
public class TurnManager {

    private final List<Player> players;
    private int currentIndex;
    private Direction direction;

    public TurnManager(List<Player> players) {
        if (players == null || players.size() < 2) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("At least 2 players are required for Ultimo");
        }
        this.players = new ArrayList<>(players);
        this.currentIndex = 0;
        this.direction = Direction.CLOCKWISE;
    }

    public Player getCurrentPlayer() {
        return players.get(currentIndex);
    }

    /**
     * Peeks at the next player in the rotation without advancing the turn.
     */
    public Player peekNextPlayer() {
        return peekNextPlayer(1);
    }

    /**
     * Peeks at the player N steps ahead in the rotation without modifying turn index.
     */
    public Player peekNextPlayer(int steps) {
        return players.get(calculateNextIndex(steps));
    }

    /**
     * Advances the turn to the next player in the current direction.
     */
    public Player advanceTurn() {
        return advanceTurn(1);
    }

    /**
     * Advances the turn by a specified number of steps in the current direction.
     */
    public Player advanceTurn(int steps) {
        currentIndex = calculateNextIndex(steps);
        return getCurrentPlayer();
    }

    /**
     * Skips the next player, advancing the turn to the player after them.
     */
    public Player skipTurn() {
        return advanceTurn(2);
    }

    /**
     * Reverses play rotation direction.
     * Note: In a 2-player match, Reverse behaves as a Skip (turn stays with the player after skip).
     */
    public void reverseDirection() {
        direction = direction.toggle();
    }

    public boolean isTwoPlayerGame() {
        return players.size() == 2;
    }

    public Direction getDirection() {
        return direction;
    }

    public List<Player> getPlayers() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(players);
    }

    public int getPlayerCount() {
        return players.size();
    }

    public int getCurrentIndex() {
        return currentIndex;
    }

    private int calculateNextIndex(int steps) {
        int total = players.size();
        int delta = steps * direction.getStep();
        return (currentIndex + (delta % total) + total) % total;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/protocol/ClientCommand.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.protocol;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Type-safe, sealed model representing all incoming client commands.
 */
public sealed interface ClientCommand {

    CommandType type();

    record Join(String username) implements ClientCommand {
        @Override
        public CommandType type() {
            return CommandType.JOIN;
        }
    }

    record Play(List<Card> cards, CardColor chosenWildColor) implements ClientCommand {
        public Play(Card card, CardColor chosenWildColor) {
            this(List.of(card), chosenWildColor);
        }

        public Card card() {
            return cards != null && !cards.isEmpty() ? cards.get(0) : null;
        }

        @Override
        public CommandType type() {
            return CommandType.PLAY;
        }
    }

    record Draw() implements ClientCommand {
        @Override
        public CommandType type() {
            return CommandType.DRAW;
        }
    }

    record Pass() implements ClientCommand {
        @Override
        public CommandType type() {
            return CommandType.PASS;
        }
    }

    record Chat(String message) implements ClientCommand {
        @Override
        public CommandType type() {
            return CommandType.CHAT;
        }
    }

    record Ping() implements ClientCommand {
        @Override
        public CommandType type() {
            return CommandType.PING;
        }
    }

    record Start() implements ClientCommand {
        @Override
        public CommandType type() {
            return CommandType.START;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/protocol/ProtocolCodec.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.protocol;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardValue;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Encodes and decodes line-based protocol messages between text wire representation
 * and strongly-typed domain commands and events.
 * Wire lines are terminated with '\n'.
 */
public final class ProtocolCodec {

    private ProtocolCodec() {
        // Utility class
    }

    /**
     * Parses a raw line received from a client socket into a strongly typed ClientCommand.
     *
     * @param rawLine line received from client (with or without trailing newline)
     * @return parsed ClientCommand
     * @throws ProtocolException if format is invalid or command is unknown
     */
    public static ClientCommand decodeCommand(String rawLine) throws ProtocolException {
        if (rawLine == null || rawLine.isBlank()) {
            throw new ProtocolException("Empty or null command line received");
        }

        String line = rawLine.trim();
        String[] parts = line.split("\\s+");
        String commandName = parts[0].toUpperCase();

        try {
            return switch (commandName) {
                case "JOIN" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("JOIN command requires a username: JOIN <username>");
                    }
                    String username = line.substring(line.indexOf(' ') + 1).trim();
                    if (username.isEmpty()) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Username cannot be empty");
                    }
                    yield new ClientCommand.Join(username);
                }
                case "PLAY" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("PLAY command requires card: PLAY <card> [chosenColor]");
                    }
                    CardColor chosenWildColor = null;
                    int cardEndIndex = parts.length;
                    if (parts.length > 2) {
                        try {
                            CardColor candidate = parseColor(parts[parts.length - 1]);
                            if (candidate.isStandardColor()) {
                                chosenWildColor = candidate;
                                cardEndIndex = parts.length - 1;
                            }
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {
                            // Last token is not a color (part of card combo tokens)
                        }
                    }

                    List<Card> cards = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (int i = 1; i < cardEndIndex; i++) {
                        String token = parts[i];
                        if (token.contains(",")) {
                            for (String subToken : token.split(",")) {
                                if (!subToken.isBlank()) {
                                    cards.add(parseCard(subToken.trim()));
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            cards.add(parseCard(token));
                        }
                    }

                    if (cards.isEmpty()) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("PLAY command requires card: PLAY <card> [chosenColor]");
                    }

                    boolean hasWild = cards.stream().anyMatch(Card::isWild);
                    if (hasWild && chosenWildColor == null) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Wild cards require a chosen color: PLAY " + parts[1] + " <RED|YELLOW|GREEN|BLUE>");
                    }

                    yield new ClientCommand.Play(cards, chosenWildColor);
                }
                case "DRAW" -> new ClientCommand.Draw();
                case "PASS" -> new ClientCommand.Pass();
                case "CHAT" -> {
                    String message = parts.length > 1 ? line.substring(line.indexOf(' ') + 1).trim() : "";
                    yield new ClientCommand.Chat(message);
                }
                case "PING" -> new ClientCommand.Ping();
                case "START" -> new ClientCommand.Start();
                default -> throw new ProtocolException("Unknown command: " + commandName);
            };
        } catch (ProtocolException pe) {
            throw pe;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ProtocolException("Failed to decode command '" + line + "': " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Serializes a ClientCommand into wire-format string terminated by '\n'.
     */
    public static String encodeCommand(ClientCommand command) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(command, "command must not be null");
        return switch (command) {
            case ClientCommand.Join join -> "JOIN " + join.username() + "\n";
            case ClientCommand.Play play -> {
                String cardsStr = play.cards().stream()
                        .map(ProtocolCodec::formatCard)
                        .collect(Collectors.joining(","));
                boolean hasWild = play.cards().stream().anyMatch(Card::isWild);
                if (hasWild && play.chosenWildColor() != null) {
                    yield "PLAY " + cardsStr + " " + play.chosenWildColor() + "\n";
                }
                yield "PLAY " + cardsStr + "\n";
            }
            case ClientCommand.Draw draw -> "DRAW\n";
            case ClientCommand.Pass pass -> "PASS\n";
            case ClientCommand.Chat chat -> "CHAT " + chat.message() + "\n";
            case ClientCommand.Ping ping -> "PING\n";
            case ClientCommand.Start start -> "START\n";
        };
    }

    /**
     * Serializes a ServerEvent into wire-format string terminated by '\n'.
     */
    public static String encodeEvent(ServerEvent event) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(event, "event must not be null");
        return switch (event) {
            case ServerEvent.Welcome welcome ->
                    "WELCOME " + welcome.assignedPlayerId() + " " + welcome.message() + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.RoomStatus roomStatus -> {
                String playerList = roomStatus.playerNames().isEmpty() ? "-" : String.join(",", roomStatus.playerNames());
                yield "ROOM_STATUS " + roomStatus.roomId() + " " + roomStatus.currentPlayers() + " "
                        + roomStatus.maxPlayers() + " " + playerList + "\n";
            }
            case ServerEvent.HandUpdate handUpdate -> {
                if (handUpdate.cards().isEmpty()) {
                    yield "HAND_UPDATE\n";
                }
                String cardsStr = handUpdate.cards().stream()
                        .map(ProtocolCodec::formatCard)
                        .collect(Collectors.joining(" "));
                yield "HAND_UPDATE " + cardsStr + "\n";
            }
            case ServerEvent.TopCard topCard ->
                    "TOP_CARD " + formatCard(topCard.card()) + " " + topCard.activeColor() + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced turn ->
                    "TURN_ANNOUNCED " + turn.activePlayerName() + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.OpponentUpdate opp ->
                    "OPPONENT_UPDATE " + opp.opponentName() + " " + opp.cardCount() + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.DrawResult draw ->
                    "DRAW_RESULT " + formatCard(draw.drawnCard()) + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.ErrorEvent err ->
                    "ERROR " + err.errorMessage() + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.ChatBroadcast chat ->
                    "CHAT_BROADCAST " + chat.sender() + " " + chat.message() + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.GameOver gameOver ->
                    "GAME_OVER " + gameOver.winnerName() + "\n";
            case ServerEvent.Pong pong ->
                    "PONG\n";
            case ServerEvent.ServerShutdown shutdown ->
                    "SERVER_SHUTDOWN " + shutdown.drainSeconds() + "\n";
        };
    }

    /**
     * Parses a raw line into a strongly typed ServerEvent.
     */
    public static ServerEvent decodeEvent(String rawLine) throws ProtocolException {
        if (rawLine == null || rawLine.isBlank()) {
            throw new ProtocolException("Empty or null event line received");
        }

        String line = rawLine.trim();
        String[] parts = line.split("\\s+");
        String eventName = parts[0].toUpperCase();

        try {
            return switch (eventName) {
                case "WELCOME" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed WELCOME event: " + line);
                    }
                    String id = parts[1];
                    String message = parts.length > 2 ? line.substring(line.indexOf(parts[2])).trim() : "";
                    yield new ServerEvent.Welcome(id, message);
                }
                case "ROOM_STATUS" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 4) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed ROOM_STATUS event: " + line);
                    }
                    String roomId = parts[1];
                    int current = Integer.parseInt(parts[2]);
                    int max = Integer.parseInt(parts[3]);
                    List<String> players = (parts.length > 4 && !"-".equals(parts[4]))
                            ? Arrays.asList(parts[4].split(","))
                            : List.of();
                    yield new ServerEvent.RoomStatus(roomId, current, max, players);
                }
                case "HAND_UPDATE" -> {
                    List<Card> cards = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (int i = 1; i < parts.length; i++) {
                        cards.add(parseCard(parts[i]));
                    }
                    yield new ServerEvent.HandUpdate(cards);
                }
                case "TOP_CARD" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 3) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed TOP_CARD event: " + line);
                    }
                    Card card = parseCard(parts[1]);
                    CardColor color = parseColor(parts[2]);
                    yield new ServerEvent.TopCard(card, color);
                }
                case "TURN_ANNOUNCED" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed TURN_ANNOUNCED event: " + line);
                    }
                    yield new ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced(parts[1]);
                }
                case "OPPONENT_UPDATE" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 3) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed OPPONENT_UPDATE event: " + line);
                    }
                    yield new ServerEvent.OpponentUpdate(parts[1], Integer.parseInt(parts[2]));
                }
                case "DRAW_RESULT" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed DRAW_RESULT event: " + line);
                    }
                    yield new ServerEvent.DrawResult(parseCard(parts[1]));
                }
                case "ERROR" -> {
                    String msg = parts.length > 1 ? line.substring(line.indexOf(' ') + 1).trim() : "Unknown error";
                    yield new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent(msg);
                }
                case "CHAT_BROADCAST" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed CHAT_BROADCAST event: " + line);
                    }
                    String sender = parts[1];
                    String msg = parts.length > 2 ? line.substring(line.indexOf(parts[2])).trim() : "";
                    yield new ServerEvent.ChatBroadcast(sender, msg);
                }
                case "GAME_OVER" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed GAME_OVER event: " + line);
                    }
                    yield new ServerEvent.GameOver(parts[1]);
                }
                case "SERVER_SHUTDOWN" -> {
                    if (parts.length < 2) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Malformed SERVER_SHUTDOWN event: " + line);
                    }
                    try {
                        yield new ServerEvent.ServerShutdown(Integer.parseInt(parts[1]));
                    } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                        throw new ProtocolException("Invalid drain timeout in SERVER_SHUTDOWN: " + parts[1]);
                    }
                }
                case "PONG" -> new ServerEvent.Pong();
                default -> throw new ProtocolException("Unknown server event: " + eventName);
            };
        } catch (ProtocolException pe) {
            throw pe;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ProtocolException("Failed to decode event '" + line + "': " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Formats a card instance into wire representation.
     */
    public static String formatCard(Card card) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(card, "card must not be null");
        if (card.value() == CardValue.WILD) {
            return "WILD";
        }
        if (card.value() == CardValue.WILD_DRAW_FOUR) {
            return "WILD_DRAW_FOUR";
        }
        return card.color().name() + "_" + card.value().name();
    }

    /**
     * Parses a card token from wire representation into a Card instance.
     * Supports formats like:
     * - "WILD", "WILD_DRAW_FOUR"
     * - "RED_FIVE", "BLUE_SKIP", "YELLOW_DRAW_TWO"
     * - "RED_5", "BLUE_0", "GREEN_DRAW2"
     */
    public static Card parseCard(String token) throws ProtocolException {
        if (token == null || token.isBlank()) {
            throw new ProtocolException("Empty card token");
        }

        String normalized = token.trim().toUpperCase();

        if ("WILD".equals(normalized) || "WILD_WILD".equals(normalized)) {
            return new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD);
        }
        if ("WILD_DRAW_FOUR".equals(normalized) || "WILD_DRAW4".equals(normalized) || "WILD_WILD_DRAW_FOUR".equals(normalized)) {
            return new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD_DRAW_FOUR);
        }

        int separator = normalized.indexOf('_');
        if (separator <= 0 || separator >= normalized.length() - 1) {
            throw new ProtocolException("Invalid card format (expected COLOR_VALUE, got: " + token + ")");
        }

        String colorPart = normalized.substring(0, separator);
        String valuePart = normalized.substring(separator + 1);

        CardColor color = parseColor(colorPart);
        CardValue value = parseValue(valuePart);

        return new Card(color, value);
    }

    private static CardColor parseColor(String token) throws ProtocolException {
        try {
            return CardColor.valueOf(token.trim().toUpperCase());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new ProtocolException("Unknown card color: " + token);
        }
    }

    private static CardValue parseValue(String token) throws ProtocolException {
        String normalized = token.trim().toUpperCase();
        return switch (normalized) {
            case "0", "ZERO" -> CardValue.ZERO;
            case "1", "ONE" -> CardValue.ONE;
            case "2", "TWO" -> CardValue.TWO;
            case "3", "THREE" -> CardValue.THREE;
            case "4", "FOUR" -> CardValue.FOUR;
            case "5", "FIVE" -> CardValue.FIVE;
            case "6", "SIX" -> CardValue.SIX;
            case "7", "SEVEN" -> CardValue.SEVEN;
            case "8", "EIGHT" -> CardValue.EIGHT;
            case "9", "NINE" -> CardValue.NINE;
            case "SKIP" -> CardValue.SKIP;
            case "REVERSE" -> CardValue.REVERSE;
            case "DRAW_TWO", "DRAW2", "DRAW_2" -> CardValue.DRAW_TWO;
            case "WILD" -> CardValue.WILD;
            case "WILD_DRAW_FOUR", "WILD_DRAW4" -> CardValue.WILD_DRAW_FOUR;
            default -> throw new ProtocolException("Unknown card value: " + token);
        };
    }
}
```

```java
package pl.ultimo.protocol;

/**
 * Thrown when an incoming network payload cannot be parsed or violates protocol framing rules.
 */
public class ProtocolException extends Exception {

    public ProtocolException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    public ProtocolException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/protocol/ServerEvent.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.protocol;

import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Type-safe, sealed model representing all outgoing server events.
 */
public sealed interface ServerEvent {

    EventType type();

    record Welcome(String assignedPlayerId, String message) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.WELCOME;
        }
    }

    record RoomStatus(String roomId, int currentPlayers, int maxPlayers, List<String> playerNames) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.ROOM_STATUS;
        }
    }

    record HandUpdate(List<Card> cards) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.HAND_UPDATE;
        }
    }

    record TopCard(Card card, CardColor activeColor) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.TOP_CARD;
        }
    }

    record TurnAnnounced(String activePlayerName) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.TURN_ANNOUNCED;
        }
    }

    record OpponentUpdate(String opponentName, int cardCount) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.OPPONENT_UPDATE;
        }
    }

    record DrawResult(Card drawnCard) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.DRAW_RESULT;
        }
    }

    record ErrorEvent(String errorMessage) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.ERROR;
        }
    }

    record ChatBroadcast(String sender, String message) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.CHAT_BROADCAST;
        }
    }

    record GameOver(String winnerName) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.GAME_OVER;
        }
    }

    record Pong() implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.PONG;
        }
    }

    record ServerShutdown(int drainSeconds) implements ServerEvent {
        @Override
        public EventType type() {
            return EventType.SERVER_SHUTDOWN;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/ServerApp.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server;

import pl.ultimo.protocol.ClientCommand;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ServerEvent;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;
import pl.ultimo.server.metrics.MetricsRegistry;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.ClientSession;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.ConnectionListener;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.SocketAcceptor;
import pl.ultimo.server.room.GameRoom;
import pl.ultimo.server.room.RoomManager;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;

/**
 * Main application bootstrap for the Ultimo backend game server.
 */
public class ServerApp implements ConnectionListener {

    private final ServerConfig config;
    private final RoomManager roomManager;
    private final ExecutorService sessionExecutor;
    private final Map<String, ClientSession> activeSessions = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final AtomicBoolean isShuttingDown = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private SocketAcceptor acceptor;
    private Thread acceptorThread;
    private Thread shutdownHook;

    public ServerApp(ServerConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
        this.roomManager = new RoomManager(config);
        this.sessionExecutor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor();
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int port = 8080;
        String envPort = System.getenv("PORT");
        if (envPort != null && !envPort.isBlank()) {
            try {
                port = Integer.parseInt(envPort.trim());
            } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
                // Keep default port
            }
        }
        if (args.length > 0) {
            try {
                port = Integer.parseInt(args[0].trim());
            } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
                // Keep resolved port
            }
        }

        ServerConfig config = ServerConfig.defaultConfiguration();
        if (port != config.port()) {
            config = new ServerConfig(port, config.defaultRoomCapacity(), config.socketReadTimeoutMillis(), config.reconnectGracePeriodSeconds(), config.shutdownDrainSeconds());
        }
        ServerApp app = new ServerApp(config);
        app.start();
    }

    public void start() {
        System.out.println("Starting Ultimo Server on port " + config.port() + "...");
        this.acceptor = new SocketAcceptor(config, this::handleNewConnection);
        this.acceptorThread = Thread.ofPlatform().name("socket-acceptor").start(acceptor);

        this.shutdownHook = new Thread(() -> gracefulShutdown(config.shutdownDrainSeconds()), "ultimo-shutdown-hook");
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(shutdownHook);
    }

    public void stop() {
        if (shutdownHook != null) {
            try {
                Runtime.getRuntime().removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook);
            } catch (IllegalStateException ignored) {
                // JVM is already terminating
            }
        }
        gracefulShutdown(0);
    }

    /**
     * Executes the Google SRE / Cloud Run coordinated graceful shutdown sequence:
     * 1. Marks readiness probe as 503 to drop load balancer ingress.
     * 2. Closes SocketAcceptor to reject any incoming TCP handshakes.
     * 3. Broadcasts SERVER_SHUTDOWN <drainSeconds> to all active client sessions.
     * 4. Drains active games within the grace period.
     * 5. Flushes and terminates all client sockets cleanly.
     *
     * @param drainSeconds countdown grace period in seconds
     */
    public void gracefulShutdown(int drainSeconds) {
        if (!isShuttingDown.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            return;
        }

        System.out.println("Initiating Ultimo Server graceful shutdown (drain window: " + drainSeconds + "s)...");

        // Step 1: Broadcast SERVER_SHUTDOWN warning to all connected sessions
        ServerEvent shutdownEvent = new ServerEvent.ServerShutdown(drainSeconds);
        for (ClientSession session : activeSessions.values()) {
            try {
                session.sendEvent(shutdownEvent);
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
            }
        }

        // Step 2: During the drain window, readiness probe returns 503 Service Unavailable,
        // allowing existing rooms and sessions to drain turns while load balancers cut traffic.
        if (drainSeconds > 0) {
            try {
                Thread.sleep(drainSeconds * 1000L);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }

        // Step 3: Reject and stop accepting any incoming connections
        if (acceptor != null) {
            acceptor.close();
        }
        if (acceptorThread != null) {
            acceptorThread.interrupt();
        }

        // Step 4: Close all client sessions and shut down executors
        for (ClientSession session : activeSessions.values()) {
            try {
                session.close();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {
            }
        }
        activeSessions.clear();

        roomManager.shutdown();
        sessionExecutor.shutdown();
        try {
            if (!sessionExecutor.awaitTermination(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                sessionExecutor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            sessionExecutor.shutdownNow();
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }

        System.out.println("Ultimo Server graceful shutdown complete.");
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isServerReady() {
        return !isShuttingDown.get() && acceptor != null && acceptor.isRunning();
    }

    public boolean isShuttingDown() {
        return isShuttingDown.get();
    }

    public int getActiveSessionCount() {
        return activeSessions.size();
    }

    public int getBoundPort() {
        return acceptor != null ? acceptor.getBoundPort() : -1;
    }

    private void handleNewConnection(Socket socket) {
        if (sessionExecutor.isShutdown()) {
            try {
                socket.close();
            } catch (IOException ignored) {
            }
            return;
        }

        try {
            String sessionId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
            ClientSession session = new ClientSession(sessionId, socket, this, config.socketReadTimeoutMillis());
            activeSessions.put(sessionId, session);
            sessionExecutor.submit(session);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            System.err.println("Failed to initialize client session: " + e.getMessage());
            try {
                socket.close();
            } catch (IOException ignored) {
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onCommandReceived(ClientSession session, ClientCommand command) {
        if (command instanceof ClientCommand.Ping) {
            session.sendEvent(new ServerEvent.Pong());
            return;
        }

        if (command instanceof ClientCommand.Join join) {
            long startNanos = System.nanoTime();
            try {
                if (session.getUsername() != null) {
                    session.sendEvent(new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent("Already joined as " + session.getUsername()));
                    return;
                }
                session.setUsername(join.username());
                session.sendEvent(new ServerEvent.Welcome(session.getSessionId(), "Welcome to Ultimo, " + join.username() + "!"));
                roomManager.matchmake(session, join.username());
                return;
            } finally {
                long durationNanos = System.nanoTime() - startNanos;
                MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordCommandProcessed("JOIN", durationNanos / 1_000_000_000.0);
            }
        }

        if (session.getUsername() == null) {
            session.sendEvent(new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent("Must send JOIN <username> before issuing other commands"));
            return;
        }

        Optional<GameRoom> roomOpt = roomManager.getRoomForSession(session.getSessionId());
        if (roomOpt.isPresent()) {
            roomOpt.get().enqueueCommand(session, command);
        } else {
            session.sendEvent(new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent("Not currently in an active game room"));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onSessionDisconnected(ClientSession session) {
        if (session != null) {
            activeSessions.remove(session.getSessionId());
        }
        roomManager.handleDisconnect(session);
    }

    public RoomManager getRoomManager() {
        return roomManager;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/config/ServerConfig.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.config;

/**
 * Immutable configuration for the Ultimo TCP server.
 *
 * @param port the TCP port to bind
 * @param defaultRoomCapacity standard maximum players per room (e.g. 4)
 * @param socketReadTimeoutMillis timeout in milliseconds for idle socket reads
 * @param reconnectGracePeriodSeconds grace period for disconnected players before forfeiting
 * @param shutdownDrainSeconds grace window in seconds for draining active games on SIGTERM
 */
public record ServerConfig(
        int port,
        int defaultRoomCapacity,
        int socketReadTimeoutMillis,
        int reconnectGracePeriodSeconds,
        int shutdownDrainSeconds
) {
    public ServerConfig(int port, int defaultRoomCapacity, int socketReadTimeoutMillis, int reconnectGracePeriodSeconds) {
        this(port, defaultRoomCapacity, socketReadTimeoutMillis, reconnectGracePeriodSeconds, 5);
    }

    public static ServerConfig defaultConfiguration() {
        return new ServerConfig(8080, 4, 60_000, 30, 5);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/metrics/MetricsRegistry.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.metrics;

import java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;
import java.lang.management.MemoryMXBean;
import java.lang.management.ThreadMXBean;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.DoubleAdder;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.LongAdder;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

/**
 * Thread-safe, zero-dependency in-memory metrics registry supporting Prometheus 
 * text exposition format (version 0.0.4).
 *
 * Implemented using lock-free standard Java concurrency primitives (LongAdder, DoubleAdder, AtomicLong)
 * to guarantee zero contention on high-throughput game loops.
 */
public class MetricsRegistry {

    public static final double[] BUCKET_THRESHOLDS = {0.001, 0.005, 0.01, 0.05, 0.1};

    private static final MetricsRegistry INSTANCE = new MetricsRegistry();

    private final AtomicLong activeTcpConnections = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong activeWebSocketConnections = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong fallbackActiveRooms = new AtomicLong(0);
    private volatile Supplier<Integer> activeRoomsSupplier = null;

    private final Map<String, LongAdder> commandsProcessed = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, LongAdder> eventsEmitted = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, LongAdder> errorsTotal = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    private final LongAdder[] latencyBuckets = new LongAdder[BUCKET_THRESHOLDS.length];
    private final LongAdder latencyCount = new LongAdder();
    private final DoubleAdder latencySum = new DoubleAdder();

    private final MemoryMXBean memoryMXBean = ManagementFactory.getMemoryMXBean();
    private final ThreadMXBean threadMXBean = ManagementFactory.getThreadMXBean();

    public MetricsRegistry() {
        for (int i = 0; i < BUCKET_THRESHOLDS.length; i++) {
            latencyBuckets[i] = new LongAdder();
        }
    }

    public static MetricsRegistry getInstance() {
        return INSTANCE;
    }

    public void recordConnectionOpened(boolean isWebSocket) {
        if (isWebSocket) {
            activeWebSocketConnections.incrementAndGet();
        } else {
            activeTcpConnections.incrementAndGet();
        }
    }

    public void recordWebSocketUpgrade() {
        activeTcpConnections.updateAndGet(val -> Math.max(0, val - 1));
        activeWebSocketConnections.incrementAndGet();
    }

    public void recordConnectionClosed(boolean isWebSocket) {
        if (isWebSocket) {
            activeWebSocketConnections.updateAndGet(val -> Math.max(0, val - 1));
        } else {
            activeTcpConnections.updateAndGet(val -> Math.max(0, val - 1));
        }
    }

    public void setActiveRoomsSupplier(Supplier<Integer> supplier) {
        this.activeRoomsSupplier = supplier;
    }

    public void setActiveRooms(long count) {
        fallbackActiveRooms.set(Math.max(0, count));
    }

    public long getActiveRooms() {
        Supplier<Integer> supplier = activeRoomsSupplier;
        if (supplier != null) {
            try {
                return Math.max(0, supplier.get());
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return fallbackActiveRooms.get();
            }
        }
        return fallbackActiveRooms.get();
    }

    public void recordCommandProcessed(String commandType, double durationSeconds) {
        String key = (commandType != null && !commandType.isBlank()) ? commandType.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT) : "UNKNOWN";
        commandsProcessed.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> new LongAdder()).increment();

        double safeDuration = Math.max(0.0, durationSeconds);
        latencyCount.increment();
        latencySum.add(safeDuration);

        for (int i = 0; i < BUCKET_THRESHOLDS.length; i++) {
            if (safeDuration <= BUCKET_THRESHOLDS[i]) {
                latencyBuckets[i].increment();
            }
        }
    }

    public void recordEventEmitted(String eventType) {
        String key = (eventType != null && !eventType.isBlank()) ? eventType.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT) : "UNKNOWN";
        eventsEmitted.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> new LongAdder()).increment();
    }

    public void recordError(String errorCode) {
        String key = (errorCode != null && !errorCode.isBlank()) ? errorCode.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT) : "UNKNOWN";
        errorsTotal.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> new LongAdder()).increment();
    }

    public long getActiveTcpConnections() {
        return activeTcpConnections.get();
    }

    public long getActiveWebSocketConnections() {
        return activeWebSocketConnections.get();
    }

    public long getCommandCount(String type) {
        LongAdder adder = commandsProcessed.get(type.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
        return adder != null ? adder.sum() : 0;
    }

    public long getEventCount(String type) {
        LongAdder adder = eventsEmitted.get(type.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
        return adder != null ? adder.sum() : 0;
    }

    public long getErrorCount(String errorCode) {
        LongAdder adder = errorsTotal.get(errorCode.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
        return adder != null ? adder.sum() : 0;
    }

    public long getLatencyBucketCount(int index) {
        if (index >= 0 && index < latencyBuckets.length) {
            return latencyBuckets[index].sum();
        }
        return 0;
    }

    public long getLatencyTotalCount() {
        return latencyCount.sum();
    }

    public double getLatencyTotalSum() {
        return latencySum.sum();
    }

    public void reset() {
        activeTcpConnections.set(0);
        activeWebSocketConnections.set(0);
        fallbackActiveRooms.set(0);
        activeRoomsSupplier = null;

        commandsProcessed.clear();
        eventsEmitted.clear();
        errorsTotal.clear();

        for (LongAdder bucket : latencyBuckets) {
            bucket.reset();
        }
        latencyCount.reset();
        latencySum.reset();
    }

    /**
     * Serializes all gathered metrics into the standard Prometheus Text Exposition Format (version 0.0.4).
     */
    public String toPrometheusText() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(2048);

        // 1. ultimo_active_connections
        sb.append("# HELP ultimo_active_connections Current active client connections by transport type.\n");
        sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_active_connections gauge\n");
        sb.append("ultimo_active_connections{transport=\"tcp\"} ").append(activeTcpConnections.get()).append("\n");
        sb.append("ultimo_active_connections{transport=\"websocket\"} ").append(activeWebSocketConnections.get()).append("\n\n");

        // 2. ultimo_active_rooms
        sb.append("# HELP ultimo_active_rooms Current number of active game rooms.\n");
        sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_active_rooms gauge\n");
        sb.append("ultimo_active_rooms ").append(getActiveRooms()).append("\n\n");

        // 3. ultimo_commands_processed_total
        sb.append("# HELP ultimo_commands_processed_total Total number of client commands processed.\n");
        sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_commands_processed_total counter\n");
        if (commandsProcessed.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append("ultimo_commands_processed_total{type=\"NONE\"} 0\n");
        } else {
            commandsProcessed.entrySet().stream()
                    .sorted(Map.Entry.comparingByKey())
                    .forEach(e -> sb.append("ultimo_commands_processed_total{type=\"")
                            .append(escapeLabelValue(e.getKey()))
                            .append("\"} ")
                            .append(e.getValue().sum())
                            .append("\n"));
        }
        sb.append("\n");

        // 4. ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds
        sb.append("# HELP ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds Latency of command processing in seconds.\n");
        sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds histogram\n");
        for (int i = 0; i < BUCKET_THRESHOLDS.length; i++) {
            sb.append("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_bucket{le=\"")
                    .append(formatBucketThreshold(BUCKET_THRESHOLDS[i]))
                    .append("\"} ")
                    .append(latencyBuckets[i].sum())
                    .append("\n");
        }
        sb.append("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_bucket{le=\"+Inf\"} ")
                .append(latencyCount.sum())
                .append("\n");
        sb.append("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_sum ")
                .append(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.6f", latencySum.sum()))
                .append("\n");
        sb.append("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_count ")
                .append(latencyCount.sum())
                .append("\n\n");

        // 5. ultimo_events_emitted_total
        if (!eventsEmitted.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append("# HELP ultimo_events_emitted_total Total number of server events emitted to clients.\n");
            sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_events_emitted_total counter\n");
            eventsEmitted.entrySet().stream()
                    .sorted(Map.Entry.comparingByKey())
                    .forEach(e -> sb.append("ultimo_events_emitted_total{type=\"")
                            .append(escapeLabelValue(e.getKey()))
                            .append("\"} ")
                            .append(e.getValue().sum())
                            .append("\n"));
            sb.append("\n");
        }

        // 6. ultimo_errors_total
        if (!errorsTotal.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append("# HELP ultimo_errors_total Total number of errors encountered.\n");
            sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_errors_total counter\n");
            errorsTotal.entrySet().stream()
                    .sorted(Map.Entry.comparingByKey())
                    .forEach(e -> sb.append("ultimo_errors_total{code=\"")
                            .append(escapeLabelValue(e.getKey()))
                            .append("\"} ")
                            .append(e.getValue().sum())
                            .append("\n"));
            sb.append("\n");
        }

        // 7. ultimo_jvm_memory_used_bytes
        long heapUsed = memoryMXBean.getHeapMemoryUsage().getUsed();
        sb.append("# HELP ultimo_jvm_memory_used_bytes Current JVM heap memory used in bytes.\n");
        sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_jvm_memory_used_bytes gauge\n");
        sb.append("ultimo_jvm_memory_used_bytes ").append(heapUsed).append("\n\n");

        // 8. ultimo_jvm_threads_current
        int threadCount = threadMXBean.getThreadCount();
        sb.append("# HELP ultimo_jvm_threads_current Current number of live JVM threads.\n");
        sb.append("# TYPE ultimo_jvm_threads_current gauge\n");
        sb.append("ultimo_jvm_threads_current ").append(threadCount).append("\n");

        return sb.toString();
    }

    private static String formatBucketThreshold(double val) {
        if (val == (long) val) {
            return String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%d", (long) val);
        }
        return String.valueOf(val);
    }

    private static String escapeLabelValue(String val) {
        return val.replace("\\", "\\\\")
                .replace("\"", "\\\"")
                .replace("\n", "\\n");
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/ClientSession.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.net;

import pl.ultimo.protocol.ClientCommand;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ProtocolCodec;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ProtocolException;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ServerEvent;
import pl.ultimo.server.metrics.MetricsRegistry;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.Closeable;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.net.SocketTimeoutException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.BlockingQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;

/**
 * Universal client session handling both Raw TCP wire socket connections and RFC 6455 WebSockets.
 * Employs non-blocking outbound queue serialization and ReentrantLock to completely eliminate
 * Project Loom Virtual Thread carrier pinning.
 */
public class ClientSession implements Runnable, Closeable {

    private final String sessionId;
    private final Socket socket;
    private final ConnectionListener listener;
    private final AtomicBoolean isClosed = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final ReentrantLock writeLock = new ReentrantLock();

    private final InputStream rawInputStream;
    private final OutputStream rawOutputStream;

    private final BlockingQueue<ServerEvent> outboundQueue = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(1024);
    private volatile Thread outboundWriterThread;
    private final int idleTimeoutMillis;
    private final AtomicLong lastActivityMillis = new AtomicLong(System.currentTimeMillis());
    private int consecutiveTimeouts = 0;

    private volatile boolean isWebSocket = false;
    private volatile String username;

    public ClientSession(String sessionId, Socket socket, ConnectionListener listener) throws IOException {
        this(sessionId, socket, listener, 60_000);
    }

    public ClientSession(String sessionId, Socket socket, ConnectionListener listener, int idleTimeoutMillis) throws IOException {
        this.sessionId = Objects.requireNonNull(sessionId, "sessionId must not be null");
        this.socket = Objects.requireNonNull(socket, "socket must not be null");
        this.listener = Objects.requireNonNull(listener, "listener must not be null");
        this.idleTimeoutMillis = idleTimeoutMillis;

        this.rawInputStream = socket.getInputStream();
        this.rawOutputStream = socket.getOutputStream();

        if (idleTimeoutMillis > 0) {
            int checkInterval = Math.max(50, idleTimeoutMillis / 2);
            socket.setSoTimeout(checkInterval);
        }

        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordConnectionOpened(false);
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            String firstLine = readAsciiLine(rawInputStream);
            if (firstLine == null) {
                return;
            }

            if (firstLine.startsWith("GET ")) {
                handleHttpOrWebSocket(firstLine);
            } else {
                if (!listener.isServerReady()) {
                    sendEvent(new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent("Server is shutting down"));
                    close();
                    return;
                }
                startOutboundWriter();
                handleTcpConnection(firstLine);
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            if (!isClosed.get() && !Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
                System.err.println("Session " + sessionId + " encountered I/O error: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        } finally {
            close();
        }
    }

    private void startOutboundWriter() {
        this.outboundWriterThread = Thread.ofVirtual().name("session-writer-" + sessionId).start(this::runOutboundLoop);
    }

    private void runOutboundLoop() {
        try {
            while (!isClosed.get()) {
                ServerEvent event = outboundQueue.poll(200, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
                if (event == null) {
                    continue;
                }
                writeEventDirectly(event);
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            if (!isClosed.get()) {
                close();
            }
        }
    }

    private void handleHttpOrWebSocket(String requestLine) throws IOException {
        Map<String, String> headers = new HashMap<>();
        String headerLine;
        while ((headerLine = readAsciiLine(rawInputStream)) != null && !headerLine.isEmpty()) {
            int colon = headerLine.indexOf(':');
            if (colon > 0) {
                String key = headerLine.substring(0, colon).trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
                String val = headerLine.substring(colon + 1).trim();
                headers.put(key, val);
            }
        }

        String upgrade = headers.get("upgrade");
        if (upgrade != null && upgrade.equalsIgnoreCase("websocket")) {
            String secKey = headers.get("sec-websocket-key");
            if (secKey != null) {
                String acceptKey = WebSocketFrameCodec.computeAcceptKey(secKey);
                String handshakeResponse = "HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols\r\n"
                        + "Upgrade: websocket\r\n"
                        + "Connection: Upgrade\r\n"
                        + "Sec-WebSocket-Accept: " + acceptKey + "\r\n\r\n";

                writeLock.lock();
                try {
                    rawOutputStream.write(handshakeResponse.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                    rawOutputStream.flush();
                } finally {
                    writeLock.unlock();
                }

                this.isWebSocket = true;
                MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordWebSocketUpgrade();
                startOutboundWriter();
                runWebSocketLoop();
                return;
            }
        }

        // Standard HTTP GET request: serve static web assets or healthcheck
        handleStaticHttpRequest(requestLine);
    }

    private void handleStaticHttpRequest(String requestLine) throws IOException {
        String path = "/";
        String[] parts = requestLine.split(" ");
        if (parts.length > 1) {
            path = parts[1];
        }

        byte[] body;
        String contentType;
        int statusCode = 200;
        String statusText = "OK";

        if (path.equals("/metrics")) {
            body = MetricsRegistry.getInstance().toPrometheusText().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            contentType = "text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8";
        } else if (path.equals("/health") || path.equals("/ping") || path.equals("/healthz")) {
            body = "OK".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            contentType = "text/plain";
        } else if (path.equals("/ready")) {
            if (listener.isServerReady()) {
                body = "OK".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                contentType = "text/plain";
            } else {
                body = "Service Unavailable".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                contentType = "text/plain";
                statusCode = 503;
                statusText = "Service Unavailable";
            }
        } else {
            body = WebClientAssets.getHtmlPage().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            contentType = "text/html; charset=UTF-8";
        }

        String responseHeader = "HTTP/1.1 " + statusCode + " " + statusText + "\r\n"
                + "Content-Type: " + contentType + "\r\n"
                + "Content-Length: " + body.length + "\r\n"
                + "Connection: close\r\n\r\n";

        writeLock.lock();
        try {
            rawOutputStream.write(responseHeader.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            rawOutputStream.write(body);
            rawOutputStream.flush();
        } finally {
            writeLock.unlock();
        }
        close();
    }

    private void runWebSocketLoop() {
        try {
            while (!isClosed.get()) {
                WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame;
                try {
                    frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(rawInputStream, true);
                } catch (WebSocketFrameCodec.WebSocketProtocolException wpe) {
                    MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordError("WS_PROTOCOL_ERROR");
                    writeLock.lock();
                    try {
                        WebSocketFrameCodec.writeCloseFrame(rawOutputStream, wpe.getStatusCode(), wpe.getMessage());
                    } catch (IOException ignored) {
                    } finally {
                        writeLock.unlock();
                    }
                    break;
                } catch (SocketTimeoutException e) {
                    if (idleTimeoutMillis > 0) {
                        consecutiveTimeouts++;
                        if (consecutiveTimeouts >= 2) {
                            System.out.println("Session " + sessionId + " idle timeout exceeded without heartbeat. Closing.");
                            break;
                        }
                        writeLock.lock();
                        try {
                            WebSocketFrameCodec.writePingFrame(rawOutputStream, new byte[0]);
                        } finally {
                            writeLock.unlock();
                        }
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (frame == null || frame.isClose()) {
                    if (frame != null && frame.isClose()) {
                        writeLock.lock();
                        try {
                            WebSocketFrameCodec.writeCloseFrame(rawOutputStream, frame.closeCode(), "");
                        } finally {
                            writeLock.unlock();
                        }
                    }
                    break;
                }

                consecutiveTimeouts = 0;
                lastActivityMillis.set(System.currentTimeMillis());

                if (frame.isPing()) {
                    writeLock.lock();
                    try {
                        WebSocketFrameCodec.writePongFrame(rawOutputStream, frame.payload());
                    } finally {
                        writeLock.unlock();
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (frame.isPong()) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (frame.isText()) {
                    String line = frame.text().trim();
                    if (!line.isEmpty()) {
                        processCommandLine(line);
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            if (!isClosed.get() && !Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
                // Connection closed normally
            }
        } finally {
            close();
        }
    }

    private void handleTcpConnection(String firstLine) throws IOException {
        processCommandLine(firstLine);

        BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(rawInputStream, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        while (!isClosed.get()) {
            String line;
            try {
                line = reader.readLine();
            } catch (SocketTimeoutException e) {
                if (idleTimeoutMillis > 0) {
                    consecutiveTimeouts++;
                    if (consecutiveTimeouts >= 2) {
                        System.out.println("Session " + sessionId + " idle timeout exceeded without heartbeat. Closing.");
                        break;
                    }
                    sendEvent(new ServerEvent.Pong());
                }
                continue;
            }

            if (line == null) {
                break;
            }

            consecutiveTimeouts = 0;
            lastActivityMillis.set(System.currentTimeMillis());

            if (line.isBlank()) {
                continue;
            }

            processCommandLine(line);
        }
    }

    private void processCommandLine(String line) {
        try {
            ClientCommand command = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand(line);
            listener.onCommandReceived(this, command);
        } catch (ProtocolException pe) {
            MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordError("PROTOCOL_ERROR");
            sendEvent(new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent("Protocol error: " + pe.getMessage()));
        }
    }

    /**
     * Sends a ServerEvent to the connected client asynchronously.
     * Enqueues to the dedicated outbound queue so that slow clients cannot stall the caller.
     */
    public void sendEvent(ServerEvent event) {
        if (isClosed.get()) {
            return;
        }

        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordEventEmitted(event.type().name());
        if (event instanceof ServerEvent.ErrorEvent) {
            MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordError("ERROR_EVENT");
        }

        Thread writer = this.outboundWriterThread;
        if (writer != null && writer.isAlive()) {
            boolean enqueued = outboundQueue.offer(event);
            if (!enqueued) {
                MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordError("QUEUE_OVERFLOW");
                System.err.println("Session " + sessionId + " outbound queue full, dropping connection.");
                close();
            }
        } else {
            try {
                writeEventDirectly(event);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                close();
            }
        }
    }

    private void writeEventDirectly(ServerEvent event) throws IOException {
        writeLock.lock();
        try {
            if (isClosed.get()) {
                return;
            }
            if (isWebSocket) {
                String wire = ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(event).trim();
                WebSocketFrameCodec.writeTextFrame(rawOutputStream, wire);
            } else {
                String wire = ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(event);
                rawOutputStream.write(wire.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                rawOutputStream.flush();
            }
        } finally {
            writeLock.unlock();
        }
    }

    private String readAsciiLine(InputStream in) throws IOException {
        ByteArrayOutputStream buffer = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        int b;
        while ((b = in.read()) != -1) {
            if (b == '\n') {
                break;
            }
            if (b != '\r') {
                buffer.write(b);
            }
        }
        if (b == -1 && buffer.size() == 0) {
            return null;
        }
        return buffer.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    public String getSessionId() {
        return sessionId;
    }

    public String getUsername() {
        return username;
    }

    public void setUsername(String username) {
        this.username = username;
    }

    public boolean isClosed() {
        return isClosed.get();
    }

    public boolean isWebSocket() {
        return isWebSocket;
    }

    public Socket getSocket() {
        return socket;
    }

    public long getLastActivityMillis() {
        return lastActivityMillis.get();
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (isClosed.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            MetricsRegistry.getInstance().recordConnectionClosed(isWebSocket);
            if (outboundWriterThread != null) {
                outboundWriterThread.interrupt();
            }
            try {
                socket.close();
            } catch (IOException ignored) {
            }
            listener.onSessionDisconnected(this);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "ClientSession{"
                + "sessionId='" + sessionId + '\''
                + ", username='" + username + '\''
                + ", isWebSocket=" + isWebSocket
                + ", closed=" + isClosed.get()
                + '}';
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/ConnectionListener.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.net;

import pl.ultimo.protocol.ClientCommand;

/**
 * Callback listener for lifecycle and messaging events received from a client session.
 */
public interface ConnectionListener {

    void onCommandReceived(ClientSession session, ClientCommand command);

    void onSessionDisconnected(ClientSession session);

    /**
     * Indicates whether the server is actively accepting connections and ready for traffic.
     * When false (e.g. during graceful drain), readiness probes (/ready) return 503.
     */
    default boolean isServerReady() {
        return true;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/SocketAcceptor.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.net;

import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;

import java.io.Closeable;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.ServerSocket;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.net.SocketException;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Listens on the configured TCP port, configures incoming client sockets,
 * and delegates each accepted connection to the configured connection handler.
 */
public class SocketAcceptor implements Runnable, Closeable {

    private final ServerConfig config;
    private final Consumer<Socket> connectionHandler;
    private final AtomicBoolean isRunning = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private ServerSocket serverSocket;

    public SocketAcceptor(ServerConfig config, Consumer<Socket> connectionHandler) {
        this.config = Objects.requireNonNull(config, "config must not be null");
        this.connectionHandler = Objects.requireNonNull(connectionHandler, "connectionHandler must not be null");
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            serverSocket = new ServerSocket(config.port());
            isRunning.set(true);

            while (isRunning.get()) {
                try {
                    Socket clientSocket = serverSocket.accept();
                    configureSocket(clientSocket);
                    connectionHandler.accept(clientSocket);
                } catch (SocketException e) {
                    if (!isRunning.get()) {
                        // Clean shutdown
                        break;
                    }
                    System.err.println("Acceptor socket error: " + e.getMessage());
                } catch (IOException e) {
                    if (isRunning.get()) {
                        System.err.println("I/O error accepting connection: " + e.getMessage());
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            System.err.println("Could not bind ServerSocket on port " + config.port() + ": " + e.getMessage());
        } finally {
            close();
        }
    }

    private void configureSocket(Socket socket) throws SocketException {
        socket.setTcpNoDelay(true); // Disable Nagle's algorithm for low-latency game packets
        if (config.socketReadTimeoutMillis() > 0) {
            socket.setSoTimeout(config.socketReadTimeoutMillis());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Returns the port the ServerSocket is bound to, or -1 if not yet bound.
     */
    public int getBoundPort() {
        return (serverSocket != null && serverSocket.isBound()) ? serverSocket.getLocalPort() : -1;
    }

    public boolean isRunning() {
        return isRunning.get();
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (isRunning.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
            if (serverSocket != null && !serverSocket.isClosed()) {
                try {
                    serverSocket.close();
                } catch (IOException ignored) {
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

```
