`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/core/model/DeckTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.model;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("Deck Domain Unit Tests")
class DeckTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Standard Ultimo deck should contain exactly 108 cards")
    void standardDeckShouldContain108Cards() {
        Deck deck = Deck.createStandardDeck();
        assertEquals(108, deck.size());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Standard deck must contain the exact distribution of Ultimo cards")
    void deckShouldHaveCorrectCardDistribution() {
        Deck deck = Deck.createStandardDeck();
        List<Card> cards = new ArrayList<>();
        while (!deck.isEmpty()) {
            cards.add(deck.draw().orElseThrow());
        }

        assertEquals(108, cards.size());

        // Count per card type
        long zeroCount = cards.stream().filter(c -> c.value() == CardValue.ZERO).count();
        long wildCount = cards.stream().filter(c -> c.value() == CardValue.WILD).count();
        long wildDrawFourCount = cards.stream().filter(c -> c.value() == CardValue.WILD_DRAW_FOUR).count();
        long skipCount = cards.stream().filter(c -> c.value() == CardValue.SKIP).count();
        long reverseCount = cards.stream().filter(c -> c.value() == CardValue.REVERSE).count();
        long drawTwoCount = cards.stream().filter(c -> c.value() == CardValue.DRAW_TWO).count();

        assertEquals(4, zeroCount, "Each of the 4 colors must have exactly one 0 card");
        assertEquals(4, wildCount, "Deck must have 4 Wild cards");
        assertEquals(4, wildDrawFourCount, "Deck must have 4 Wild Draw Four cards");
        assertEquals(8, skipCount, "Deck must have 8 Skip cards (2 per color)");
        assertEquals(8, reverseCount, "Deck must have 8 Reverse cards (2 per color)");
        assertEquals(8, drawTwoCount, "Deck must have 8 Draw Two cards (2 per color)");

        // 72 cards of values 1-9 (2 each per color * 9 * 4)
        long oneToNineCount = cards.stream()
                .filter(c -> c.value().isNumber() && c.value() != CardValue.ZERO)
                .count();
        assertEquals(72, oneToNineCount);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Drawing all cards should deplete the deck")
    void drawingCardsShouldDepleteDeck() {
        Deck deck = Deck.createStandardDeck();
        for (int i = 0; i < 108; i++) {
            assertTrue(deck.draw().isPresent());
        }
        assertTrue(deck.isEmpty());
        assertEquals(Optional.empty(), deck.draw());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Replenish from discard should refill an empty deck")
    void replenishFromDiscardRefillsDeck() {
        Deck deck = new Deck();
        assertTrue(deck.isEmpty());

        List<Card> discarded = List.of(
                new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.ONE),
                new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.FIVE)
        );

        deck.replenishFromDiscard(discarded);
        assertEquals(2, deck.size());
        assertFalse(deck.isEmpty());
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/core/state/TurnManagerTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.core.state;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Player;

import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("TurnManager Domain Unit Tests")
class TurnManagerTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Turn should advance sequentially in clockwise direction")
    void turnShouldAdvanceClockwise() {
        Player alice = new Player("1", "Alice");
        Player bob = new Player("2", "Bob");
        Player charlie = new Player("3", "Charlie");

        TurnManager turnManager = new TurnManager(List.of(alice, bob, charlie));
        assertEquals("Alice", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());

        turnManager.advanceTurn();
        assertEquals("Bob", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());

        turnManager.advanceTurn();
        assertEquals("Charlie", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());

        turnManager.advanceTurn();
        assertEquals("Alice", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Skip turn should skip next player and give turn to player after")
    void skipTurnShouldSkipPlayer() {
        Player alice = new Player("1", "Alice");
        Player bob = new Player("2", "Bob");
        Player charlie = new Player("3", "Charlie");

        TurnManager turnManager = new TurnManager(List.of(alice, bob, charlie));
        assertEquals("Alice", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());

        turnManager.skipTurn();
        assertEquals("Charlie", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername(), "Bob should have been skipped");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Reverse direction should switch from clockwise to counter-clockwise")
    void reverseDirectionShouldCycleBackwards() {
        Player alice = new Player("1", "Alice");
        Player bob = new Player("2", "Bob");
        Player charlie = new Player("3", "Charlie");

        TurnManager turnManager = new TurnManager(List.of(alice, bob, charlie));
        assertEquals(Direction.CLOCKWISE, turnManager.getDirection());

        turnManager.reverseDirection();
        assertEquals(Direction.COUNTER_CLOCKWISE, turnManager.getDirection());

        // Moving counter-clockwise from Alice (index 0) in 3 players lands on Charlie (index 2)
        turnManager.advanceTurn();
        assertEquals("Charlie", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());

        turnManager.advanceTurn();
        assertEquals("Bob", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Peek next player should not modify current player index")
    void peekNextPlayerShouldNotChangeTurn() {
        Player alice = new Player("1", "Alice");
        Player bob = new Player("2", "Bob");

        TurnManager turnManager = new TurnManager(List.of(alice, bob));
        assertEquals("Alice", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());

        Player peeked = turnManager.peekNextPlayer();
        assertEquals("Bob", peeked.getUsername());
        assertEquals("Alice", turnManager.getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("TurnManager requires at least two players")
    void shouldThrowWhenFewerThanTwoPlayers() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new TurnManager(List.of(new Player("1", "Solo"))));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new TurnManager(List.of()));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new TurnManager(null));
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/protocol/ProtocolCodecTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.protocol;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardValue;

import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("ProtocolCodec Unit Tests")
class ProtocolCodecTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode valid JOIN command")
    void shouldDecodeJoinCommand() throws ProtocolException {
        ClientCommand command = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("JOIN Alice");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Join.class, command);
        assertEquals("Alice", ((ClientCommand.Join) command).username());

        // Multi-word username
        ClientCommand multiWord = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("JOIN Alice The Great");
        assertEquals("Alice The Great", ((ClientCommand.Join) multiWord).username());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should throw ProtocolException when JOIN lacks username")
    void shouldThrowWhenJoinLacksUsername() {
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("JOIN"));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("JOIN   "));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode PLAY command with standard card")
    void shouldDecodePlayStandardCard() throws ProtocolException {
        // Name-based
        ClientCommand cmdWord = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY RED_FIVE");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Play.class, cmdWord);
        ClientCommand.Play playWord = (ClientCommand.Play) cmdWord;
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.FIVE), playWord.card());
        assertNull(playWord.chosenWildColor());

        // Digit-based
        ClientCommand cmdDigit = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY BLUE_5");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Play.class, cmdDigit);
        ClientCommand.Play playDigit = (ClientCommand.Play) cmdDigit;
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.FIVE), playDigit.card());

        // Action card
        ClientCommand cmdSkip = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY GREEN_SKIP");
        ClientCommand.Play playSkip = (ClientCommand.Play) cmdSkip;
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.GREEN, CardValue.SKIP), playSkip.card());

        // Draw two with alternate notation
        ClientCommand cmdDraw2 = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY YELLOW_DRAW2");
        ClientCommand.Play playDraw2 = (ClientCommand.Play) cmdDraw2;
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.YELLOW, CardValue.DRAW_TWO), playDraw2.card());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode PLAY command with Wild card and declared color")
    void shouldDecodePlayWildCard() throws ProtocolException {
        ClientCommand cmdWild = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY WILD BLUE");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Play.class, cmdWild);
        ClientCommand.Play playWild = (ClientCommand.Play) cmdWild;
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD), playWild.card());
        assertEquals(CardColor.BLUE, playWild.chosenWildColor());

        ClientCommand cmdWildDraw4 = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY WILD_DRAW4 RED");
        ClientCommand.Play playWildDraw4 = (ClientCommand.Play) cmdWildDraw4;
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD_DRAW_FOUR), playWildDraw4.card());
        assertEquals(CardColor.RED, playWildDraw4.chosenWildColor());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should throw ProtocolException when Wild card played without declaring color")
    void shouldThrowWhenWildPlayedWithoutColor() {
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY WILD"));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY WILD_DRAW_FOUR"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode DRAW, PASS, PING, and START commands")
    void shouldDecodeSimpleCommands() throws ProtocolException {
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Draw.class, ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("DRAW"));
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Draw.class, ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("  draw  \r\n"));

        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Pass.class, ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PASS"));
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Ping.class, ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PING"));
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Start.class, ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("START"));
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Start.class, ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("  start  \n"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode CHAT command with full message payload")
    void shouldDecodeChatCommand() throws ProtocolException {
        ClientCommand cmd = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("CHAT Hello all, good luck!");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Chat.class, cmd);
        assertEquals("Hello all, good luck!", ((ClientCommand.Chat) cmd).message());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should throw ProtocolException for empty line or unknown command")
    void shouldThrowOnMalformedInput() {
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand(""));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("   \n"));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand(null));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("DANCE"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should encode ClientCommands to wire format")
    void shouldEncodeClientCommands() {
        assertEquals("JOIN Bob\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Join("Bob")));
        assertEquals("PLAY RED_SEVEN\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Play(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.SEVEN), null)));
        assertEquals("PLAY WILD YELLOW\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Play(new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD), CardColor.YELLOW)));
        assertEquals("DRAW\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Draw()));
        assertEquals("PASS\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Pass()));
        assertEquals("PING\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Ping()));
        assertEquals("START\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Start()));
        assertEquals("CHAT Hi!\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(new ClientCommand.Chat("Hi!")));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should encode ServerEvents to wire format")
    void shouldEncodeServerEvents() {
        assertEquals("WELCOME id-1 Hello!\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.Welcome("id-1", "Hello!")));
        assertEquals("ROOM_STATUS r-1 2 4 Alice,Bob\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.RoomStatus("r-1", 2, 4, List.of("Alice", "Bob"))));
        assertEquals("TOP_CARD RED_FIVE RED\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.TopCard(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.FIVE), CardColor.RED)));
        assertEquals("TURN_ANNOUNCED Alice\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced("Alice")));
        assertEquals("OPPONENT_UPDATE Bob 4\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.OpponentUpdate("Bob", 4)));
        assertEquals("ERROR Bad move\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent("Bad move")));
        assertEquals("PONG\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.Pong()));
        assertEquals("SERVER_SHUTDOWN 5\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(new ServerEvent.ServerShutdown(5)));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should perform lossless round-trip serialization of ServerEvents")
    void shouldRoundTripServerEvents() throws ProtocolException {
        List<ServerEvent> testEvents = List.of(
                new ServerEvent.Welcome("player-1", "Welcome to Ultimo server"),
                new ServerEvent.RoomStatus("room-42", 3, 4, List.of("Alice", "Bob", "Charlie")),
                new ServerEvent.HandUpdate(List.of(
                        new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.ZERO),
                        new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.SKIP),
                        new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD)
                )),
                new ServerEvent.TopCard(new Card(CardColor.GREEN, CardValue.DRAW_TWO), CardColor.GREEN),
                new ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced("Charlie"),
                new ServerEvent.OpponentUpdate("Alice", 6),
                new ServerEvent.DrawResult(new Card(CardColor.YELLOW, CardValue.NINE)),
                new ServerEvent.ErrorEvent("Not your turn to play!"),
                new ServerEvent.ChatBroadcast("Alice", "Well played Bob"),
                new ServerEvent.GameOver("Bob"),
                new ServerEvent.Pong(),
                new ServerEvent.ServerShutdown(5)
        );

        for (ServerEvent event : testEvents) {
            String wire = ProtocolCodec.encodeEvent(event);
            ServerEvent decoded = ProtocolCodec.decodeEvent(wire);
            assertEquals(event, decoded, "Failed round-trip for event: " + event.type());
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Card parsing should validate token format")
    void cardParsingValidatesTokenFormat() {
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.parseCard(""));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.parseCard("INVALID"));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.parseCard("PURPLE_FIVE"));
        assertThrows(ProtocolException.class, () -> ProtocolCodec.parseCard("RED_ELEVEN"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode multi-card PLAY command with comma-separated cards")
    void shouldDecodeMultiCardCommaSeparated() throws ProtocolException {
        ClientCommand cmd = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY RED_FIVE,BLUE_FIVE");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Play.class, cmd);
        ClientCommand.Play play = (ClientCommand.Play) cmd;
        assertEquals(2, play.cards().size());
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.FIVE), play.cards().get(0));
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.FIVE), play.cards().get(1));
        assertNull(play.chosenWildColor());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode multi-card PLAY command with space-separated cards")
    void shouldDecodeMultiCardSpaceSeparated() throws ProtocolException {
        ClientCommand cmd = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY RED_FIVE BLUE_FIVE");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Play.class, cmd);
        ClientCommand.Play play = (ClientCommand.Play) cmd;
        assertEquals(2, play.cards().size());
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.FIVE), play.cards().get(0));
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.FIVE), play.cards().get(1));
        assertNull(play.chosenWildColor());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should decode multi-card PLAY command with Wild cards and declared color")
    void shouldDecodeMultiCardWithWildCards() throws ProtocolException {
        ClientCommand cmd = ProtocolCodec.decodeCommand("PLAY WILD,WILD GREEN");
        assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Play.class, cmd);
        ClientCommand.Play play = (ClientCommand.Play) cmd;
        assertEquals(2, play.cards().size());
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD), play.cards().get(0));
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.WILD, CardValue.WILD), play.cards().get(1));
        assertEquals(CardColor.GREEN, play.chosenWildColor());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should encode multi-card PLAY command to wire format")
    void shouldEncodeMultiCardPlayCommand() {
        ClientCommand.Play play = new ClientCommand.Play(
                List.of(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.FIVE), new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.FIVE)),
                null
        );
        assertEquals("PLAY RED_FIVE,BLUE_FIVE\n", ProtocolCodec.encodeCommand(play));
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/ConcurrencyAndPinningTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;
import pl.ultimo.server.metrics.MetricsRegistry;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

@DisplayName("Virtual Thread Pinning Elimination & Concurrency Hardening Tests")
class ConcurrencyAndPinningTest {

    private ServerApp serverApp;
    private int port;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws InterruptedException {
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (serverApp != null) {
            serverApp.stop();
        }
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
    }

    private void startServer(int readTimeoutMillis) throws InterruptedException {
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 4, readTimeoutMillis, 30, 1);
        serverApp = new ServerApp(config);
        serverApp.start();

        int attempts = 0;
        while ((port = serverApp.getBoundPort()) <= 0 && attempts++ < 50) {
            Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        assertTrue(port > 0, "Server failed to bind to a random free port");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Dedicated outbound queue ensures slow network clients do not delay fast clients")
    void shouldNotBlockFastClientsWhenSlowClientHasLatency() throws Exception {
        startServer(60_000);

        // Connect 3 clients
        Socket fastSocket1 = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
        Socket fastSocket2 = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
        Socket slowSocket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);

        BufferedWriter fastOut1 = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(fastSocket1.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        BufferedReader fastIn1 = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(fastSocket1.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        BufferedWriter fastOut2 = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(fastSocket2.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        BufferedReader fastIn2 = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(fastSocket2.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        BufferedWriter slowOut = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(slowSocket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        BufferedReader slowIn = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(slowSocket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        // Join fast clients
        fastOut1.write("JOIN FastPlayer1\n");
        fastOut1.flush();
        fastIn1.readLine(); // WELCOME
        fastIn1.readLine(); // ROOM_STATUS

        fastOut2.write("JOIN FastPlayer2\n");
        fastOut2.flush();
        fastIn2.readLine(); // WELCOME
        fastIn2.readLine(); // ROOM_STATUS
        fastIn1.readLine(); // ROOM_STATUS (player 2 joined)

        // Join slow client
        slowOut.write("JOIN SlowPlayer\n");
        slowOut.flush();
        fastIn1.readLine(); // ROOM_STATUS (player 3 joined)
        fastIn2.readLine(); // ROOM_STATUS (player 3 joined)

        // Slow client does NOT read anything right away - simulates high latency / stalled consumer
        // Now FastPlayer1 sends a CHAT message to the room
        long startNanos = System.nanoTime();
        fastOut1.write("CHAT Hello room\n");
        fastOut1.flush();

        // FastPlayer2 should receive the chat message nearly instantaneously (< 200ms)
        // regardless of whether SlowPlayer is reading from their socket buffer
        String chatMsg = fastIn2.readLine();
        long elapsedMillis = (System.nanoTime() - startNanos) / 1_000_000;

        assertNotNull(chatMsg);
        assertTrue(chatMsg.contains("CHAT_BROADCAST FastPlayer1 Hello room"),
                "Fast client did not receive expected chat, got: " + chatMsg);
        assertTrue(elapsedMillis < 300, "Fast client was delayed by slow client: " + elapsedMillis + "ms");

        // Now SlowPlayer finally reads their buffered frames
        String slowWelcome = slowIn.readLine();
        assertNotNull(slowWelcome);
        assertTrue(slowWelcome.startsWith("WELCOME"));

        fastSocket1.close();
        fastSocket2.close();
        slowSocket.close();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Idle socket watchdog reaps dead connections when heartbeat goes unacknowledged")
    void shouldActivelyReapIdleConnectionsWhenHeartbeatUnacknowledged() throws Exception {
        // Configure short idle timeout of 400ms for testing watchdog (checkInterval = 200ms)
        startServer(400);

        Socket idleSocket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
        BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(idleSocket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(idleSocket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        writer.write("JOIN IdlePlayer\n");
        writer.flush();

        String welcome = reader.readLine();
        assertNotNull(welcome);
        assertTrue(welcome.startsWith("WELCOME"));
        reader.readLine(); // ROOM_STATUS

        // Verify session is tracked in activeSessions
        assertEquals(1, serverApp.getActiveSessionCount(), "Client session was not tracked");

        // Client now goes completely silent without sending any commands or replying
        // Server will timeout after 200ms (sends PONG probe) and after another 200ms will close the connection
        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        boolean socketClosedByServer = false;

        try {
            String line;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                // Client may observe PONG probe
            }
            socketClosedByServer = true;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            socketClosedByServer = true;
        }

        long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;
        assertTrue(socketClosedByServer, "Server did not close the idle socket");
        assertTrue(elapsed >= 250, "Watchdog closed too early: " + elapsed + "ms");

        // Verify active session count dropped to 0
        int waitAttempts = 0;
        while (serverApp.getActiveSessionCount() > 0 && waitAttempts++ < 30) {
            Thread.sleep(30);
        }
        assertEquals(0, serverApp.getActiveSessionCount(), "Dead session was not removed from active sessions");

        idleSocket.close();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Server responds to PING immediately without requiring JOIN")
    void shouldRespondToClientPingsWithoutRequiringJoin() throws Exception {
        startServer(60_000);

        try (Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
             BufferedWriter out = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(socket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
             BufferedReader in = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(socket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {

            out.write("PING\n");
            out.flush();

            String response = in.readLine();
            assertNotNull(response);
            assertEquals("PONG", response.trim());
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Verify zero synchronized monitor locks in pl.ultimo.server package")
    void shouldHaveZeroSynchronizedKeywordsInServerPackage() throws IOException {
        Path serverPackagePath = Path.of("src", "main", "java", "pl", "ultimo", "server");
        assertTrue(Files.exists(serverPackagePath), "Server package directory must exist");

        List<String> violations = new ArrayList<>();
        try (Stream<Path> paths = Files.walk(serverPackagePath)) {
            paths.filter(Files::isRegularFile)
                 .filter(p -> p.toString().endsWith(".java"))
                 .forEach(path -> {
                     try {
                         List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(path);
                         for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
                             String line = lines.get(i);
                             String trimmed = line.trim();
                             if (trimmed.startsWith("//") || trimmed.startsWith("*")) {
                                 continue;
                             }
                             if (line.contains("synchronized")) {
                                 violations.add(path.getFileName() + ":" + (i + 1) + " -> " + trimmed);
                             }
                         }
                     } catch (IOException e) {
                         throw new RuntimeException(e);
                     }
                 });
        }

        assertTrue(violations.isEmpty(),
                "Found synchronized keyword in server package (violates Virtual Thread carrier non-pinning requirement):\n"
                        + String.join("\n", violations));
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/EndToEndGameFlowTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ClientCommand;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ProtocolCodec;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ServerEvent;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("End-to-End Server and Multiplayer Gameplay Test")
class EndToEndGameFlowTest {

    private ServerApp server;
    private int port;

    private static class RemotePlayerClient implements AutoCloseable {
        private final Socket socket;
        private final BufferedWriter writer;
        private final BufferedReader reader;
        private final List<ServerEvent> receivedEvents = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());

        public RemotePlayerClient(int port) throws Exception {
            this.socket = new Socket("localhost", port);
            this.writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(socket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            this.reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(socket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }

        public void send(String line) throws Exception {
            writer.write(line.endsWith("\n") ? line : line + "\n");
            writer.flush();
        }

        public ServerEvent readEvent(long timeoutMillis) throws Exception {
            long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMillis;
            while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
                if (reader.ready()) {
                    String line = reader.readLine();
                    if (line != null && !line.isBlank()) {
                        ServerEvent event = ProtocolCodec.decodeEvent(line);
                        receivedEvents.add(event);
                        return event;
                    }
                }
                Thread.sleep(20);
            }
            return null;
        }

        public void drainEvents() {
            try {
                while (reader.ready()) {
                    String line = reader.readLine();
                    if (line != null && !line.isBlank()) {
                        receivedEvents.add(ProtocolCodec.decodeEvent(line));
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }

        public <T extends ServerEvent> T waitForEvent(Class<T> type, long timeoutMillis) throws Exception {
            long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMillis;
            while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
                drainEvents();
                for (ServerEvent ev : receivedEvents) {
                    if (type.isInstance(ev)) {
                        return type.cast(ev);
                    }
                }
                Thread.sleep(20);
            }
            return null;
        }

        @Override
        public void close() throws Exception {
            socket.close();
        }
    }

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws InterruptedException {
        // Room capacity 2 for immediate auto-start
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 2, 10_000, 10);
        server = new ServerApp(config);
        server.start();

        int retries = 50;
        while ((port = server.getBoundPort()) <= 0 && retries-- > 0) {
            Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        assertTrue(port > 0, "Server failed to bind");
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (server != null) {
            server.stop();
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Complete end-to-end flow: connection, lobby joining, game start, and card play")
    void fullEndToEndGameFlow() throws Exception {
        try (RemotePlayerClient alice = new RemotePlayerClient(port);
             RemotePlayerClient bob = new RemotePlayerClient(port)) {

            // 1. Alice joins
            alice.send("JOIN Alice");
            ServerEvent.Welcome aliceWelcome = alice.waitForEvent(ServerEvent.Welcome.class, 2000);
            assertNotNull(aliceWelcome);
            assertTrue(aliceWelcome.message().contains("Alice"));

            // 2. Bob joins -> triggers auto-match start!
            bob.send("JOIN Bob");
            ServerEvent.Welcome bobWelcome = bob.waitForEvent(ServerEvent.Welcome.class, 2000);
            assertNotNull(bobWelcome);
            assertTrue(bobWelcome.message().contains("Bob"));

            // 3. Both receive game start events
            ServerEvent.TopCard topCardEvent = alice.waitForEvent(ServerEvent.TopCard.class, 2000);
            assertNotNull(topCardEvent, "Alice should have received initial TOP_CARD");

            ServerEvent.HandUpdate aliceHandEvent = alice.waitForEvent(ServerEvent.HandUpdate.class, 2000);
            assertNotNull(aliceHandEvent, "Alice should have received private HAND_UPDATE");
            assertEquals(7, aliceHandEvent.cards().size());

            ServerEvent.HandUpdate bobHandEvent = bob.waitForEvent(ServerEvent.HandUpdate.class, 2000);
            assertNotNull(bobHandEvent, "Bob should have received private HAND_UPDATE");
            assertEquals(7, bobHandEvent.cards().size());

            // 4. Verify turn announcement
            ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced turnEvent = alice.waitForEvent(ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced.class, 2000);
            assertNotNull(turnEvent);
            String activePlayer = turnEvent.activePlayerName();
            assertTrue(activePlayer.equals("Alice") || activePlayer.equals("Bob"));

            // 5. Active player performs a DRAW command
            RemotePlayerClient activeClient = activePlayer.equals("Alice") ? alice : bob;
            activeClient.send("DRAW");

            ServerEvent.DrawResult drawResult = activeClient.waitForEvent(ServerEvent.DrawResult.class, 2000);
            assertNotNull(drawResult, "Active player should have received DRAW_RESULT");

            // Active player passes
            activeClient.send("PASS");
            Thread.sleep(200);

            // 6. Turn successfully advanced to the next player
            alice.drainEvents();
            bob.drainEvents();
            boolean gotNextTurn = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                    .filter(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced)
                    .count() >= 2;
            assertTrue(gotNextTurn, "Turn should have advanced after pass");
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/GracefulShutdownTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;
import pl.ultimo.server.metrics.MetricsRegistry;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.net.ConnectException;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

@DisplayName("Google SRE Graceful Shutdown & Health Probe Lifecycle Tests")
class GracefulShutdownTest {

    private ServerApp serverApp;
    private int port;
    private HttpClient httpClient;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws InterruptedException {
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
        // 2-second drain window for testing
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 2, 5000, 30, 2);
        serverApp = new ServerApp(config);
        serverApp.start();

        int attempts = 0;
        while ((port = serverApp.getBoundPort()) <= 0 && attempts++ < 50) {
            Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        assertTrue(port > 0, "Server failed to bind to a random free port");

        httpClient = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (serverApp != null) {
            serverApp.stop();
        }
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should transition /ready probe from 200 OK to 503 Service Unavailable when shutdown begins")
    void shouldTransitionReadinessProbeOnShutdown() throws Exception {
        // Step 1: When running, /ready returns 200 OK
        HttpRequest readyReq = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/ready"))
                .GET()
                .build();
        HttpResponse<String> initialRes = httpClient.send(readyReq, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
        assertEquals(200, initialRes.statusCode());
        assertEquals("OK", initialRes.body().trim());

        // Step 2: Trigger graceful shutdown in the background with 2s drain
        CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> serverApp.gracefulShutdown(2));

        // Allow shutdown signal to set isShuttingDown flag and close acceptor
        Thread.sleep(150);

        assertTrue(serverApp.isShuttingDown());
        assertFalse(serverApp.isServerReady());

        // Step 3: Readiness probe must immediately fail with 503 so Cloud Run / K8s drops ingress
        HttpResponse<String> drainingRes = httpClient.send(readyReq, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
        assertEquals(503, drainingRes.statusCode());
        assertTrue(drainingRes.body().contains("Service Unavailable"));

        // Step 4: Liveness probe /healthz remains 200 OK while process is draining
        HttpRequest livenessReq = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/healthz"))
                .GET()
                .build();
        HttpResponse<String> livenessRes = httpClient.send(livenessReq, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
        assertEquals(200, livenessRes.statusCode());
        assertEquals("OK", livenessRes.body().trim());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should broadcast SERVER_SHUTDOWN to connected clients and cleanly terminate sockets")
    void shouldBroadcastShutdownToClientsAndDrainCleanly() throws Exception {
        try (Socket clientSocket = new Socket("localhost", port);
             BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(clientSocket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
             BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(clientSocket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {

            // Register client
            writer.write("JOIN Alice\n");
            writer.flush();

            String welcome = reader.readLine();
            assertNotNull(welcome);
            assertTrue(welcome.startsWith("WELCOME"));

            assertEquals(1, serverApp.getActiveSessionCount());

            // Initiate graceful shutdown with 1s drain window in background
            CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> serverApp.gracefulShutdown(1));

            // Read lines until SERVER_SHUTDOWN 1 is received
            String line;
            String shutdownEvent = null;
            while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                if (line.startsWith("SERVER_SHUTDOWN")) {
                    shutdownEvent = line;
                    break;
                }
            }
            assertNotNull(shutdownEvent, "Client must receive broadcast SERVER_SHUTDOWN event");
            assertTrue(shutdownEvent.startsWith("SERVER_SHUTDOWN 1"),
                    "Expected SERVER_SHUTDOWN 1, got: " + shutdownEvent);

            // Wait for 1s drain window to elapse plus buffer
            Thread.sleep(1300);

            // Socket must now be cleanly closed by server (reader returns null EOF)
            String postDrain = reader.readLine();
            assertNull(postDrain, "Socket must be closed after graceful drain completes");
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should reject new incoming TCP connections after shutdown is triggered")
    void shouldRejectNewConnectionsDuringShutdown() throws Exception {
        // Trigger shutdown immediately
        serverApp.stop();

        assertTrue(serverApp.isShuttingDown());

        // Attempting to connect to the bound port should fail or immediately close
        try (Socket socket = new Socket("localhost", port)) {
            socket.setSoTimeout(500);
            BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(socket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            // Either connection is refused or reader immediately returns EOF
            String line = reader.readLine();
            assertNull(line);
        } catch (IOException expected) {
            // Expected socket error when acceptor is closed
            assertTrue(true);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/metrics/MetricsEndpointIntegrationTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.metrics;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.server.ServerApp;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

@DisplayName("Prometheus Metrics & Health Probe Integration Tests")
class MetricsEndpointIntegrationTest {

    private ServerApp serverApp;
    private int port;
    private HttpClient httpClient;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws InterruptedException {
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 2, 5000, 30);
        serverApp = new ServerApp(config);
        serverApp.start();

        int attempts = 0;
        while ((port = serverApp.getBoundPort()) <= 0 && attempts++ < 50) {
            Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        assertTrue(port > 0, "Server failed to bind to a random free port");

        httpClient = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (serverApp != null) {
            serverApp.stop();
        }
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should respond 200 OK with Prometheus exposition format on GET /metrics")
    void testGetMetricsEndpoint() throws Exception {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/metrics"))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response = httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
        assertEquals("text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8",
                response.headers().firstValue("Content-Type").orElse(""));

        String body = response.body();
        assertNotNull(body);
        assertTrue(body.contains("# HELP ultimo_active_connections"), "Must declare active_connections");
        assertTrue(body.contains("# TYPE ultimo_active_connections gauge"));
        assertTrue(body.contains("# HELP ultimo_active_rooms"));
        assertTrue(body.contains("# HELP ultimo_commands_processed_total"));
        assertTrue(body.contains("# HELP ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds"));
        assertTrue(body.contains("# HELP ultimo_jvm_memory_used_bytes"));
        assertTrue(body.contains("# HELP ultimo_jvm_threads_current"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should respond 200 OK on GET /healthz and GET /ready")
    void testHealthAndReadyProbes() throws Exception {
        HttpRequest livenessReq = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/healthz"))
                .GET()
                .build();
        HttpResponse<String> livenessRes = httpClient.send(livenessReq, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
        assertEquals(200, livenessRes.statusCode());
        assertEquals("OK", livenessRes.body().trim());

        HttpRequest readinessReq = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/ready"))
                .GET()
                .build();
        HttpResponse<String> readinessRes = httpClient.send(readinessReq, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
        assertEquals(200, readinessRes.statusCode());
        assertEquals("OK", readinessRes.body().trim());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should reflect real gameplay commands in Prometheus metrics scrape")
    void testMetricsReflectGameplay() throws Exception {
        // Connect a TCP socket and issue JOIN Alice
        try (Socket socket = new Socket("localhost", port);
             BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(socket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
             BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(socket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {

            writer.write("JOIN Alice\n");
            writer.flush();

            String welcome = reader.readLine();
            assertNotNull(welcome);
            assertTrue(welcome.startsWith("WELCOME"));

            // Scrape /metrics via HTTP
            HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                    .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/metrics"))
                    .GET()
                    .build();

            HttpResponse<String> response = httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
            assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());

            String body = response.body();
            assertTrue(body.contains("ultimo_commands_processed_total{type=\"JOIN\"} 1"),
                    "Should have recorded 1 JOIN command in Prometheus counter");
            assertTrue(body.contains("ultimo_events_emitted_total{type=\"WELCOME\"} 1"),
                    "Should have recorded 1 WELCOME event in Prometheus counter");
            assertTrue(body.contains("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_count 1"),
                    "Should have recorded 1 observation in command duration histogram");
        }
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/metrics/MetricsRegistryTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.metrics;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

@DisplayName("MetricsRegistry Unit & Concurrency Tests")
class MetricsRegistryTest {

    private MetricsRegistry registry;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        registry = new MetricsRegistry();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should initialize gauges with default zero values")
    void testInitialGauges() {
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveTcpConnections());
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveWebSocketConnections());
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveRooms());
        assertEquals(0, registry.getLatencyTotalCount());
        assertEquals(0.0, registry.getLatencyTotalSum(), 0.0001);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should track connection open, upgrade to WebSocket, and close")
    void testConnectionLifecycle() {
        registry.recordConnectionOpened(false);
        registry.recordConnectionOpened(false);
        assertEquals(2, registry.getActiveTcpConnections());
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveWebSocketConnections());

        registry.recordWebSocketUpgrade();
        assertEquals(1, registry.getActiveTcpConnections());
        assertEquals(1, registry.getActiveWebSocketConnections());

        registry.recordConnectionClosed(true);
        assertEquals(1, registry.getActiveTcpConnections());
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveWebSocketConnections());

        registry.recordConnectionClosed(false);
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveTcpConnections());
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveWebSocketConnections());

        // Guard against negative counts
        registry.recordConnectionClosed(false);
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveTcpConnections());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should track active rooms via dynamic supplier and fallback")
    void testActiveRoomsSupplier() {
        registry.setActiveRooms(5);
        assertEquals(5, registry.getActiveRooms());

        List<String> mockRooms = new ArrayList<>(List.of("room1", "room2"));
        registry.setActiveRoomsSupplier(mockRooms::size);
        assertEquals(2, registry.getActiveRooms());

        mockRooms.add("room3");
        assertEquals(3, registry.getActiveRooms());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should accumulate cumulative histogram buckets correctly")
    void testHistogramCumulativeBuckets() {
        // Record 1 latency of 0.0005s (should fall in <=0.001, <=0.005, <=0.01, <=0.05, <=0.1, +Inf)
        registry.recordCommandProcessed("PLAY", 0.0005);

        // Record 1 latency of 0.003s (should fall in <=0.005, <=0.01, <=0.05, <=0.1, +Inf)
        registry.recordCommandProcessed("PLAY", 0.003);

        // Record 1 latency of 0.02s (should fall in <=0.05, <=0.1, +Inf)
        registry.recordCommandProcessed("DRAW", 0.02);

        // Record 1 latency of 0.5s (should fall ONLY in +Inf)
        registry.recordCommandProcessed("PASS", 0.5);

        assertEquals(4, registry.getLatencyTotalCount());
        assertEquals(0.5235, registry.getLatencyTotalSum(), 0.0001);

        // Buckets: [0.001, 0.005, 0.01, 0.05, 0.1]
        assertEquals(1, registry.getLatencyBucketCount(0)); // <= 0.001 (0.0005)
        assertEquals(2, registry.getLatencyBucketCount(1)); // <= 0.005 (0.0005, 0.003)
        assertEquals(2, registry.getLatencyBucketCount(2)); // <= 0.01 (0.0005, 0.003)
        assertEquals(3, registry.getLatencyBucketCount(3)); // <= 0.05 (0.0005, 0.003, 0.02)
        assertEquals(3, registry.getLatencyBucketCount(4)); // <= 0.1 (0.0005, 0.003, 0.02)

        assertEquals(2, registry.getCommandCount("PLAY"));
        assertEquals(1, registry.getCommandCount("DRAW"));
        assertEquals(1, registry.getCommandCount("PASS"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should track event emitted and error counts")
    void testEventsAndErrors() {
        registry.recordEventEmitted("WELCOME");
        registry.recordEventEmitted("WELCOME");
        registry.recordEventEmitted("TOP_CARD");
        assertEquals(2, registry.getEventCount("WELCOME"));
        assertEquals(1, registry.getEventCount("TOP_CARD"));
        assertEquals(0, registry.getEventCount("GAME_OVER"));

        registry.recordError("PROTOCOL_ERROR");
        registry.recordError("INVALID_MOVE");
        assertEquals(1, registry.getErrorCount("PROTOCOL_ERROR"));
        assertEquals(1, registry.getErrorCount("INVALID_MOVE"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should export valid Prometheus Text Exposition 0.0.4 format")
    void testToPrometheusText() {
        registry.recordConnectionOpened(false);
        registry.recordConnectionOpened(true);
        registry.setActiveRooms(2);
        registry.recordCommandProcessed("JOIN", 0.002);
        registry.recordEventEmitted("WELCOME");
        registry.recordError("PROTOCOL_ERROR");

        String exposition = registry.toPrometheusText();
        assertNotNull(exposition);

        // Assert SRE required metrics are present
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("# HELP ultimo_active_connections"), "Must declare active_connections HELP");
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("# TYPE ultimo_active_connections gauge"), "Must declare active_connections TYPE");
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_active_connections{transport=\"tcp\"} 1"));
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_active_connections{transport=\"websocket\"} 1"));

        assertTrue(exposition.contains("# HELP ultimo_active_rooms"), "Must declare active_rooms HELP");
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_active_rooms 2"));

        assertTrue(exposition.contains("# HELP ultimo_commands_processed_total"), "Must declare commands_processed HELP");
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_commands_processed_total{type=\"JOIN\"} 1"));

        assertTrue(exposition.contains("# HELP ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds"), "Must declare duration HELP");
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_bucket{le=\"0.005\"} 1"));
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_bucket{le=\"+Inf\"} 1"));
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_command_processing_duration_seconds_count 1"));

        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_events_emitted_total{type=\"WELCOME\"} 1"));
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_errors_total{code=\"PROTOCOL_ERROR\"} 1"));

        assertTrue(exposition.contains("# HELP ultimo_jvm_memory_used_bytes"), "Must include JVM memory HELP");
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_jvm_memory_used_bytes "));
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("# HELP ultimo_jvm_threads_current"), "Must include JVM threads HELP");
        assertTrue(exposition.contains("ultimo_jvm_threads_current "));

        // Prometheus requires text to terminate with newline
        assertTrue(exposition.endsWith("\n"), "Prometheus text must terminate with trailing newline");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should handle highly concurrent metric updates without data race")
    void testConcurrency() throws Exception {
        int threads = 20;
        int iterations = 1000;
        ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(threads);

        List<Callable<Void>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < threads; i++) {
            tasks.add(() -> {
                for (int j = 0; j < iterations; j++) {
                    registry.recordConnectionOpened(false);
                    registry.recordWebSocketUpgrade();
                    registry.recordCommandProcessed("PLAY", 0.002);
                    registry.recordEventEmitted("HAND_UPDATE");
                    registry.recordConnectionClosed(true);
                }
                return null;
            });
        }

        List<Future<Void>> futures = executor.invokeAll(tasks);
        for (Future<Void> future : futures) {
            future.get();
        }
        executor.shutdown();

        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveTcpConnections());
        assertEquals(0, registry.getActiveWebSocketConnections());
        assertEquals(threads * iterations, registry.getCommandCount("PLAY"));
        assertEquals(threads * iterations, registry.getEventCount("HAND_UPDATE"));
        assertEquals(threads * iterations, registry.getLatencyTotalCount());
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/NetworkLayerTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.net;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ClientCommand;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ProtocolCodec;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ServerEvent;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.BufferedWriter;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("Network Layer and Concurrency Integration Tests")
class NetworkLayerTest {

    private SocketAcceptor acceptor;
    private Thread acceptorThread;
    private ExecutorService virtualThreadExecutor;
    private int serverPort;

    private final List<ClientCommand> receivedCommands = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
    private final List<ClientSession> disconnectedSessions = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
    private final AtomicReference<ClientSession> activeServerSession = new AtomicReference<>();
    private CountDownLatch commandLatch;
    private CountDownLatch disconnectLatch;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws InterruptedException {
        virtualThreadExecutor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor();
        receivedCommands.clear();
        disconnectedSessions.clear();
        activeServerSession.set(null);
        commandLatch = new CountDownLatch(1);
        disconnectLatch = new CountDownLatch(1);

        // Bind to ephemeral port 0
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 4, 10_000, 10);
        ConnectionListener listener = new ConnectionListener() {
            @Override
            public void onCommandReceived(ClientSession session, ClientCommand command) {
                receivedCommands.add(command);
                commandLatch.countDown();
            }

            @Override
            public void onSessionDisconnected(ClientSession session) {
                disconnectedSessions.add(session);
                disconnectLatch.countDown();
            }
        };

        acceptor = new SocketAcceptor(config, socket -> {
            try {
                ClientSession session = new ClientSession("session-1", socket, listener);
                activeServerSession.set(session);
                virtualThreadExecutor.submit(session);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                fail("Failed to create ClientSession: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });

        acceptorThread = Thread.ofPlatform().name("test-acceptor").start(acceptor);

        // Wait for server to bind
        int retries = 50;
        while ((serverPort = acceptor.getBoundPort()) <= 0 && retries-- > 0) {
            Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        assertTrue(serverPort > 0, "Acceptor failed to bind to a local port");
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (acceptor != null) {
            acceptor.close();
        }
        if (acceptorThread != null) {
            acceptorThread.interrupt();
        }
        if (virtualThreadExecutor != null) {
            virtualThreadExecutor.shutdownNow();
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Client can connect and exchange messages bi-directionally over TCP")
    void clientCanConnectAndExchangeMessages() throws Exception {
        try (Socket clientSocket = new Socket("localhost", serverPort);
             BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(clientSocket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
             BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(clientSocket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {

            // Client sends JOIN command
            writer.write("JOIN Alice\n");
            writer.flush();

            assertTrue(commandLatch.await(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS), "Server did not receive command in time");
            assertEquals(1, receivedCommands.size());
            assertInstanceOf(ClientCommand.Join.class, receivedCommands.getFirst());
            assertEquals("Alice", ((ClientCommand.Join) receivedCommands.getFirst()).username());

            // Server replies with WELCOME event
            ClientSession serverSession = activeServerSession.get();
            assertNotNull(serverSession);
            serverSession.sendEvent(new ServerEvent.Welcome("p-1", "Welcome to test!"));

            // Client receives WELCOME event
            String responseLine = reader.readLine();
            assertNotNull(responseLine);
            ServerEvent event = ProtocolCodec.decodeEvent(responseLine);
            assertInstanceOf(ServerEvent.Welcome.class, event);
            ServerEvent.Welcome welcome = (ServerEvent.Welcome) event;
            assertEquals("p-1", welcome.assignedPlayerId());
            assertEquals("Welcome to test!", welcome.message());
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Invalid wire protocol command produces an ERROR event back to client without crashing session")
    void invalidCommandProducesErrorEvent() throws Exception {
        try (Socket clientSocket = new Socket("localhost", serverPort);
             BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(clientSocket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
             BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(clientSocket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {

            // Send malformed / unknown command
            writer.write("MALFORMED_COMMAND\n");
            writer.flush();

            String responseLine = reader.readLine();
            assertNotNull(responseLine);
            ServerEvent event = ProtocolCodec.decodeEvent(responseLine);
            assertInstanceOf(ServerEvent.ErrorEvent.class, event);
            assertTrue(((ServerEvent.ErrorEvent) event).errorMessage().contains("Unknown command"));
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Client disconnect triggers session cleanup and disconnection callback")
    void clientDisconnectTriggersCleanup() throws Exception {
        Socket clientSocket = new Socket("localhost", serverPort);
        BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(clientSocket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        writer.write("PING\n");
        writer.flush();
        assertTrue(commandLatch.await(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS));

        // Close socket from client side
        clientSocket.close();

        assertTrue(disconnectLatch.await(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS), "Server did not detect disconnect in time");
        assertEquals(1, disconnectedSessions.size());
        assertTrue(disconnectedSessions.getFirst().isClosed());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Virtual threads can handle multiple concurrent client connections simultaneously")
    void virtualThreadsHandleConcurrentClients() throws Exception {
        int clientCount = 5;
        CountDownLatch allConnectedLatch = new CountDownLatch(clientCount);
        List<Socket> clientSockets = new ArrayList<>();

        for (int i = 0; i < clientCount; i++) {
            final int index = i;
            virtualThreadExecutor.submit(() -> {
                try {
                    Socket socket = new Socket("localhost", serverPort);
                    synchronized (clientSockets) {
                        clientSockets.add(socket);
                    }
                    BufferedWriter writer = new BufferedWriter(new OutputStreamWriter(socket.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                    writer.write("JOIN Player_" + index + "\n");
                    writer.flush();
                    allConnectedLatch.countDown();
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    fail("Concurrent client failed: " + e.getMessage());
                }
            });
        }

        assertTrue(allConnectedLatch.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS), "Not all clients connected in time");

        // Wait briefly for all commands to be processed
        Thread.sleep(500);
        assertTrue(receivedCommands.size() >= clientCount);

        // Clean up client sockets
        for (Socket s : clientSockets) {
            s.close();
        }
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/WebSocketFrameCodecTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.net;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("WebSocketFrameCodec Unit Tests (RFC 6455)")
class WebSocketFrameCodecTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should correctly calculate Sec-WebSocket-Accept according to RFC 6455 official test vector")
    void shouldComputeOfficialRfcAcceptKey() {
        // Official RFC 6455 Section 4.2.2 test vector
        String clientKey = "dGhlIHNhbXBsZSBub25jZQ==";
        String expectedAccept = "s3pPLMBiTxaQ9kYGzzhZRbK+xOo=";

        String actualAccept = WebSocketFrameCodec.computeAcceptKey(clientKey);

        assertEquals(expectedAccept, actualAccept);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should encode and decode unmasked text frame")
    void shouldEncodeAndDecodeTextFrame() throws Exception {
        String message = "JOIN Alice";
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();

        WebSocketFrameCodec.writeTextFrame(out, message);
        byte[] frameBytes = out.toByteArray();

        // Server frame: FIN=1, Opcode=1 (0x81), MASK=0, len=10
        assertEquals((byte) 0x81, frameBytes[0]);
        assertEquals((byte) 10, frameBytes[1]);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);
        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in);

        assertNotNull(frame);
        assertTrue(frame.isText());
        assertEquals(message, frame.text());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should correctly decode masked client text frame")
    void shouldDecodeMaskedClientFrame() throws Exception {
        String payloadText = "PLAY RED_7";
        byte[] payloadBytes = payloadText.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        byte[] mask = new byte[] {0x12, 0x34, 0x56, 0x78};

        ByteArrayOutputStream raw = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        raw.write(0x81); // FIN + text opcode
        raw.write(0x80 | payloadBytes.length); // MASK bit = 1 + length
        raw.write(mask); // 4-byte mask

        for (int i = 0; i < payloadBytes.length; i++) {
            raw.write(payloadBytes[i] ^ mask[i % 4]);
        }

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(raw.toByteArray());
        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in);

        assertNotNull(frame);
        assertTrue(frame.isText());
        assertEquals("PLAY RED_7", frame.text());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should encode medium text frame with 16-bit extended length (126 <= len <= 65535)")
    void shouldEncodeMediumTextFrame() throws Exception {
        String mediumText = "A".repeat(300);
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();

        WebSocketFrameCodec.writeTextFrame(out, mediumText);
        byte[] frameBytes = out.toByteArray();

        // Byte 0: 0x81, Byte 1: 126, Byte 2-3: length in big endian (300 = 0x012C)
        assertEquals((byte) 0x81, frameBytes[0]);
        assertEquals((byte) 126, frameBytes[1]);
        assertEquals((byte) 0x01, frameBytes[2]);
        assertEquals((byte) 0x2C, frameBytes[3]);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);
        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in);

        assertNotNull(frame);
        assertEquals(300, frame.text().length());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should encode and decode close frame")
    void shouldHandleCloseFrame() throws Exception {
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        WebSocketFrameCodec.writeCloseFrame(out);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(out.toByteArray());
        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in);

        assertNotNull(frame);
        assertTrue(frame.isClose());
    }
}
```
