`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/WebSocketFuzzTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.net;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;
import pl.ultimo.server.ServerApp;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;
import pl.ultimo.server.metrics.MetricsRegistry;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.WebSocketFrameCodec.WebSocketProtocolException;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.EOFException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Random;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

@DisplayName("RFC 6455 WebSocket Engine Security & Fuzz-Proof Hardening Tests")
class WebSocketFuzzTest {

    private ServerApp serverApp;
    private int port;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (serverApp != null) {
            serverApp.stop();
        }
        MetricsRegistry.getInstance().reset();
    }

    private void startServer() throws InterruptedException {
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 4, 5000, 30, 1);
        serverApp = new ServerApp(config);
        serverApp.start();

        int attempts = 0;
        while ((port = serverApp.getBoundPort()) <= 0 && attempts++ < 50) {
            Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        assertTrue(port > 0, "Server failed to bind to a random free port");
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. Client Frame Masking Enforcement (RFC 6455 §5.1)
    // =========================================================================

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "Unmasked opcode 0x{0} must be rejected with 1002")
    @ValueSource(ints = {
            WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_TEXT,
            WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_BINARY,
            WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_PING,
            WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_CLOSE
    })
    @DisplayName("Client sending unmasked frames must be rejected with status code 1002")
    void shouldRejectUnmaskedClientFrames(int opcode) {
        byte[] payload = "Hello".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        byte[] frameBytes = createFrame(true, 0, opcode, false, payload);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, ex.getStatusCode());
        assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("unmasked"));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. Invalid & Reserved Opcode Validation (RFC 6455 §5.2)
    // =========================================================================

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "Invalid reserved opcode 0x{0} must trigger 1002")
    @ValueSource(ints = {0x3, 0x4, 0x5, 0x6, 0x7, 0xB, 0xC, 0xD, 0xE, 0xF})
    @DisplayName("Frames with reserved opcodes must throw status code 1002")
    void shouldRejectReservedOpcodes(int reservedOpcode) {
        byte[] payload = new byte[]{0x01, 0x02};
        byte[] frameBytes = createFrame(true, 0, reservedOpcode, true, payload);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, ex.getStatusCode());
        assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("reserved opcode") || ex.getMessage().contains("Invalid"));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. RSV Bits Enforcement (RFC 6455 §5.2)
    // =========================================================================

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "Non-zero RSV {0} must trigger 1002")
    @ValueSource(ints = {1, 2, 4, 7})
    @DisplayName("Frames with non-zero RSV bits without negotiated extension must throw status code 1002")
    void shouldRejectNonZeroRsvBits(int rsv) {
        byte[] payload = "test".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        byte[] frameBytes = createFrame(true, rsv, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_TEXT, true, payload);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, ex.getStatusCode());
        assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("RSV bits must be 0"));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 4. Control Frame Constraints (RFC 6455 §5.5)
    // =========================================================================

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "Fragmented control frame (FIN=0) opcode 0x{0} must trigger 1002")
    @ValueSource(ints = {
            WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_PING,
            WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_PONG,
            WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_CLOSE
    })
    @DisplayName("Fragmented control frames (FIN=0) must be rejected with status code 1002")
    void shouldRejectFragmentedControlFrames(int controlOpcode) {
        byte[] payload = new byte[]{0x01};
        // FIN = false
        byte[] frameBytes = createFrame(false, 0, controlOpcode, true, payload);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, ex.getStatusCode());
        assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("Control frames must not be fragmented"));
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "Control frame payload > 125 (len={0}) must trigger 1002")
    @ValueSource(ints = {126, 150, 200})
    @DisplayName("Control frames with payload > 125 bytes must be rejected with status code 1002")
    void shouldRejectControlFramesWithExcessivePayload(int payloadLength) {
        byte[] payload = new byte[payloadLength];
        byte[] frameBytes = createFrame(true, 0, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_PING, true, payload);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, ex.getStatusCode());
        assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("exceeds 125 bytes"));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 5. Oversized Payload DOS & OOM Protection (RFC 6455 §7.4.1 / 1009)
    // =========================================================================

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Payload exceeding MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE (64KB + 1) must be rejected with status code 1009")
    void shouldRejectPayloadExceeding64Kb() {
        // Create frame header indicating 65537 bytes length
        byte[] headerBytes = createFrameHeader(true, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_TEXT, true, 65537L);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(headerBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_MESSAGE_TOO_BIG, ex.getStatusCode());
        assertTrue(ex.getMessage().contains("exceeds 64KB limit"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Payload header with 100,000 bytes must be rejected with status code 1009")
    void shouldRejectPayloadWith100000Bytes() {
        byte[] headerBytes = createFrameHeader(true, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_BINARY, true, 100_000L);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(headerBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_MESSAGE_TOO_BIG, ex.getStatusCode());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("64-bit excessive payload length (1GB) must be rejected with status code 1009 without allocating memory")
    void shouldRejectExcessive64BitPayloadLength() {
        byte[] headerBytes = createFrameHeader(true, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_TEXT, true, 1024L * 1024L * 1024L);

        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(headerBytes);
        WebSocketProtocolException ex = assertThrows(WebSocketProtocolException.class,
                () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));

        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_MESSAGE_TOO_BIG, ex.getStatusCode());
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 6. Truncated Streams & Incomplete Frame Headers
    // =========================================================================

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Clean EOF on first byte returns null without exception")
    void shouldReturnNullOnCleanEof() throws Exception {
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]);
        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true);
        assertNull(frame);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Truncated 1-byte stream throws EOFException")
    void shouldThrowEofOn1ByteHeader() {
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[]{(byte) 0x81});
        assertThrows(EOFException.class, () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Truncated 16-bit extended length throws EOFException")
    void shouldThrowEofOnTruncated16BitLength() {
        // Header says 16-bit length (126), but only 1 byte of length follows
        byte[] truncated = new byte[]{(byte) 0x81, (byte) (0x80 | 126), 0x01};
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(truncated);
        assertThrows(EOFException.class, () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Truncated 64-bit extended length throws EOFException")
    void shouldThrowEofOnTruncated64BitLength() {
        // Header says 64-bit length (127), but only 4 bytes of length follow
        byte[] truncated = new byte[]{(byte) 0x81, (byte) (0x80 | 127), 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01};
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(truncated);
        assertThrows(EOFException.class, () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Truncated mask key (only 2 bytes) throws EOFException")
    void shouldThrowEofOnTruncatedMaskKey() {
        // Mask bit set, length 5, but only 2 mask bytes follow
        byte[] truncated = new byte[]{(byte) 0x81, (byte) (0x80 | 5), 0x11, 0x22};
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(truncated);
        assertThrows(EOFException.class, () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Truncated payload bytes throws EOFException")
    void shouldThrowEofOnTruncatedPayload() {
        // Masked, length 10, valid 4-byte mask, but only 3 payload bytes follow
        byte[] truncated = new byte[]{
                (byte) 0x81, (byte) (0x80 | 10),
                0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44,
                0x01, 0x02, 0x03
        };
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(truncated);
        assertThrows(EOFException.class, () -> WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 7. Legitimate Frame Parsing & Close Code Decoders
    // =========================================================================

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Valid masked Ping frame with 0 payload is accepted")
    void shouldAcceptValidMaskedPingWithZeroPayload() throws Exception {
        byte[] pingBytes = createFrame(true, 0, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_PING, true, new byte[0]);
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(pingBytes);

        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true);
        assertNotNull(frame);
        assertTrue(frame.isPing());
        assertEquals(0, frame.payload().length);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Valid masked Close frame with status code 1000 and reason string is decoded")
    void shouldDecodeCloseFrameWithStatusCodeAndReason() throws Exception {
        byte[] reason = "Client closing normally".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        byte[] payload = new byte[2 + reason.length];
        payload[0] = (byte) ((1000 >> 8) & 0xFF);
        payload[1] = (byte) (1000 & 0xFF);
        System.arraycopy(reason, 0, payload, 2, reason.length);

        byte[] closeBytes = createFrame(true, 0, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_CLOSE, true, payload);
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(closeBytes);

        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, true);
        assertNotNull(frame);
        assertTrue(frame.isClose());
        assertEquals(1000, frame.closeCode());
        assertEquals("Client closing normally", frame.closeReason());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("writeCloseFrame formats status code in network byte order")
    void shouldFormatCloseFrameWithStatusCode() throws Exception {
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        WebSocketFrameCodec.writeCloseFrame(out, WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, "Protocol violation");

        byte[] frameBytes = out.toByteArray();
        ByteArrayInputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(frameBytes);

        // Server-sent frame is unmasked
        WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame frame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, false);
        assertNotNull(frame);
        assertTrue(frame.isClose());
        assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, frame.closeCode());
        assertEquals("Protocol violation", frame.closeReason());
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 8. End-to-End Live Socket Adversarial & Fuzzing Tests
    // =========================================================================

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Live Server: Client sending unmasked frame receives Close 1002 and socket is closed")
    void shouldCloseLiveConnectionWith1002WhenClientSendsUnmaskedFrame() throws Exception {
        startServer();

        try (Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
             OutputStream out = socket.getOutputStream();
             InputStream in = socket.getInputStream()) {

            // Step 1: Perform valid WebSocket handshake
            performWebSocketHandshake(in, out);

            // Step 2: Send UNMASKED frame (illegal according to RFC 6455 §5.1)
            byte[] unmaskedFrame = createFrame(true, 0, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_TEXT, false, "JOIN Alice".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            out.write(unmaskedFrame);
            out.flush();

            // Step 3: Server must respond with Close frame carrying status 1002
            WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame responseFrame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, false);
            assertNotNull(responseFrame, "Server did not reply with Close frame");
            assertTrue(responseFrame.isClose(), "Expected Close frame from server");
            assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, responseFrame.closeCode());

            // Server terminates socket
            int nextByte = in.read();
            assertEquals(-1, nextByte, "Server did not close the connection after protocol error");
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Live Server: Client sending fragmented control frame receives Close 1002 and socket is closed")
    void shouldCloseLiveConnectionWith1002WhenClientSendsFragmentedControlFrame() throws Exception {
        startServer();

        try (Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
             OutputStream out = socket.getOutputStream();
             InputStream in = socket.getInputStream()) {

            performWebSocketHandshake(in, out);

            // Send fragmented Ping (FIN = false)
            byte[] fragmentedPing = createFrame(false, 0, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_PING, true, new byte[0]);
            out.write(fragmentedPing);
            out.flush();

            WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame responseFrame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, false);
            assertNotNull(responseFrame);
            assertTrue(responseFrame.isClose());
            assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_PROTOCOL_ERROR, responseFrame.closeCode());

            assertEquals(-1, in.read());
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Live Server: Client sending frame header > 64KB receives Close 1009 and socket is closed")
    void shouldCloseLiveConnectionWith1009WhenClientSendsOversizedFrameHeader() throws Exception {
        startServer();

        try (Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
             OutputStream out = socket.getOutputStream();
             InputStream in = socket.getInputStream()) {

            performWebSocketHandshake(in, out);

            // Send frame header indicating 100KB payload
            byte[] oversizedHeader = createFrameHeader(true, WebSocketFrameCodec.OPCODE_TEXT, true, 100_000L);
            out.write(oversizedHeader);
            out.flush();

            WebSocketFrameCodec.Frame responseFrame = WebSocketFrameCodec.readFrame(in, false);
            assertNotNull(responseFrame);
            assertTrue(responseFrame.isClose());
            assertEquals(WebSocketFrameCodec.CLOSE_MESSAGE_TOO_BIG, responseFrame.closeCode());

            assertEquals(-1, in.read());
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Live Server: Random byte fuzzing stream terminates cleanly without server crash")
    void shouldHandleRandomByteFuzzingWithoutCrashingServer() throws Exception {
        startServer();

        Random random = new Random(42);
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            try (Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
                 OutputStream out = socket.getOutputStream();
                 InputStream in = socket.getInputStream()) {

                performWebSocketHandshake(in, out);

                // Feed 256 bytes of pure pseudorandom garbage
                byte[] garbage = new byte[256];
                random.nextBytes(garbage);

                out.write(garbage);
                out.flush();

                // Consume whatever the server sends until EOF
                byte[] buf = new byte[512];
                while (in.read(buf) != -1) {
                    // Drain
                }
            }
        }

        // Assert server is still healthy and operational after fuzz attacks
        assertTrue(serverApp.isServerReady());
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Helper Methods
    // =========================================================================

    private void performWebSocketHandshake(InputStream in, OutputStream out) throws Exception {
        String handshake = "GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n"
                + "Host: localhost:" + port + "\r\n"
                + "Upgrade: websocket\r\n"
                + "Connection: Upgrade\r\n"
                + "Sec-WebSocket-Key: dGhlIHNhbXBsZSBub25jZQ==\r\n"
                + "Sec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n\r\n";

        out.write(handshake.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        out.flush();

        // Read HTTP 101 Switching Protocols
        ByteArrayOutputStream headerBuf = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        int b;
        while ((b = in.read()) != -1) {
            headerBuf.write(b);
            String current = headerBuf.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            if (current.endsWith("\r\n\r\n")) {
                break;
            }
        }
        String response = headerBuf.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        assertTrue(response.startsWith("HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols"), "Handshake failed: " + response);
    }

    private static byte[] createFrame(boolean fin, int rsv, int opcode, boolean masked, byte[] payload) {
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        int b0 = (fin ? 0x80 : 0x00) | ((rsv & 0x07) << 4) | (opcode & 0x0F);
        out.write(b0);

        int len = payload != null ? payload.length : 0;
        int maskBit = masked ? 0x80 : 0x00;

        if (len < 126) {
            out.write(maskBit | len);
        } else if (len <= 0xFFFF) {
            out.write(maskBit | 126);
            out.write((len >> 8) & 0xFF);
            out.write(len & 0xFF);
        } else {
            out.write(maskBit | 127);
            for (int i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
                out.write((int) ((len >>> (i * 8)) & 0xFF));
            }
        }

        if (masked) {
            byte[] mask = new byte[]{0x11, 0x22, 0x33, 0x44};
            out.write(mask, 0, 4);
            if (payload != null) {
                for (int i = 0; i < payload.length; i++) {
                    out.write(payload[i] ^ mask[i % 4]);
                }
            }
        } else {
            if (payload != null) {
                out.write(payload, 0, payload.length);
            }
        }

        return out.toByteArray();
    }

    private static byte[] createFrameHeader(boolean fin, int opcode, boolean masked, long payloadLength) {
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        int b0 = (fin ? 0x80 : 0x00) | (opcode & 0x0F);
        out.write(b0);

        int maskBit = masked ? 0x80 : 0x00;
        if (payloadLength < 126) {
            out.write(maskBit | (int) payloadLength);
        } else if (payloadLength <= 0xFFFF) {
            out.write(maskBit | 126);
            out.write((int) ((payloadLength >> 8) & 0xFF));
            out.write((int) (payloadLength & 0xFF));
        } else {
            out.write(maskBit | 127);
            for (int i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
                out.write((int) ((payloadLength >>> (i * 8)) & 0xFF));
            }
        }
        if (masked) {
            out.write(new byte[]{0x01, 0x02, 0x03, 0x04}, 0, 4);
        }
        return out.toByteArray();
    }
}

```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/net/WebSocketIntegrationTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.net;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.core.engine.UltimoEngine;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardValue;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Player;
import pl.ultimo.server.ServerApp;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;
import pl.ultimo.server.room.GameRoom;

import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.net.http.WebSocket;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletionStage;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("WebSocket & HTTP Integration Tests")
class WebSocketIntegrationTest {

    private ServerApp server;
    private int port;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws Exception {
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 4, 60_000, 30);
        server = new ServerApp(config);
        server.start();

        long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 3000;
        while (server.getBoundPort() <= 0 && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
            Thread.sleep(50);
        }
        port = server.getBoundPort();
        assertTrue(port > 0, "Server failed to bind dynamic port");
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (server != null) {
            server.stop();
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should serve static HTML5 web client on HTTP GET /")
    void shouldServeStaticWebPage() throws Exception {
        HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/"))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
        assertTrue(response.body().contains("<!DOCTYPE html>"));
        assertTrue(response.body().contains("Ultimo Multiplayer | Web Edition"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should respond 200 OK to healthcheck probe on HTTP GET /health")
    void shouldRespondToHealthcheck() throws Exception {
        HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:" + port + "/health"))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response = client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
        assertEquals("OK", response.body());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should establish WebSocket connection and handle JOIN command")
    void shouldHandleWebSocketClientConnection() throws Exception {
        HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
        List<String> receivedMessages = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
        CountDownLatch welcomeLatch = new CountDownLatch(1);

        WebSocket.Listener listener = new WebSocket.Listener() {
            @Override
            public CompletionStage<?> onText(WebSocket webSocket, CharSequence data, boolean last) {
                String msg = data.toString();
                receivedMessages.add(msg);
                if (msg.startsWith("WELCOME")) {
                    welcomeLatch.countDown();
                }
                return WebSocket.Listener.super.onText(webSocket, data, last);
            }
        };

        WebSocket ws = client.newWebSocketBuilder()
                .buildAsync(URI.create("ws://localhost:" + port + "/ws"), listener)
                .get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        ws.sendText("JOIN WebAlice\n", true);

        boolean receivedWelcome = welcomeLatch.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertTrue(receivedWelcome, "Expected WELCOME message over WebSocket within 5 seconds");
        assertTrue(receivedMessages.stream().anyMatch(m -> m.contains("Welcome to Ultimo, WebAlice!")));

        ws.sendClose(WebSocket.NORMAL_CLOSURE, "Goodbye").get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should start match between two WebSocket players when START command is issued")
    void shouldStartMatchBetweenTwoWebSocketPlayersWithStartCommand() throws Exception {
        HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
        List<String> aliceMsgs = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
        List<String> bobMsgs = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());

        CountDownLatch startLatch = new CountDownLatch(2);

        WebSocket wsAlice = client.newWebSocketBuilder()
                .buildAsync(URI.create("ws://localhost:" + port + "/ws"), new WebSocket.Listener() {
                    @Override
                    public CompletionStage<?> onText(WebSocket ws, CharSequence data, boolean last) {
                        String msg = data.toString();
                        aliceMsgs.add(msg);
                        if (msg.startsWith("HAND_UPDATE")) {
                            startLatch.countDown();
                        }
                        return WebSocket.Listener.super.onText(ws, data, last);
                    }
                })
                .get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        WebSocket wsBob = client.newWebSocketBuilder()
                .buildAsync(URI.create("ws://localhost:" + port + "/ws"), new WebSocket.Listener() {
                    @Override
                    public CompletionStage<?> onText(WebSocket ws, CharSequence data, boolean last) {
                        String msg = data.toString();
                        bobMsgs.add(msg);
                        if (msg.startsWith("HAND_UPDATE")) {
                            startLatch.countDown();
                        }
                        return WebSocket.Listener.super.onText(ws, data, last);
                    }
                })
                .get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        wsAlice.sendText("JOIN Alice\n", true);
        wsBob.sendText("JOIN Bob\n", true);

        Thread.sleep(200);

        // Alice sends START to commence 2-player match
        wsAlice.sendText("START\n", true);

        boolean started = startLatch.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertTrue(started, "Expected both players to receive HAND_UPDATE after START command");

        // Verify game state events arrived for both players
        assertTrue(aliceMsgs.stream().anyMatch(m -> m.startsWith("TOP_CARD")));
        assertTrue(aliceMsgs.stream().anyMatch(m -> m.startsWith("TURN_ANNOUNCED")));
        assertTrue(bobMsgs.stream().anyMatch(m -> m.startsWith("TOP_CARD")));
        assertTrue(bobMsgs.stream().anyMatch(m -> m.startsWith("TURN_ANNOUNCED")));

        wsAlice.sendClose(WebSocket.NORMAL_CLOSURE, "Bye");
        wsBob.sendClose(WebSocket.NORMAL_CLOSURE, "Bye");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Should play multi-card combo over WebSocket and broadcast effects")
    void shouldPlayMultiCardComboOverWebSocket() throws Exception {
        HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
        List<String> aliceMsgs = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
        List<String> bobMsgs = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());

        CountDownLatch startLatch = new CountDownLatch(2);
        CountDownLatch comboChatLatch = new CountDownLatch(2);

        WebSocket wsAlice = client.newWebSocketBuilder()
                .buildAsync(URI.create("ws://localhost:" + port + "/ws"), new WebSocket.Listener() {
                    @Override
                    public CompletionStage<?> onText(WebSocket ws, CharSequence data, boolean last) {
                        String msg = data.toString();
                        aliceMsgs.add(msg);
                        if (msg.startsWith("HAND_UPDATE")) {
                            startLatch.countDown();
                        }
                        if (msg.startsWith("CHAT") && msg.contains("played a combo of 2 cards!")) {
                            comboChatLatch.countDown();
                        }
                        return WebSocket.Listener.super.onText(ws, data, last);
                    }
                })
                .get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        WebSocket wsBob = client.newWebSocketBuilder()
                .buildAsync(URI.create("ws://localhost:" + port + "/ws"), new WebSocket.Listener() {
                    @Override
                    public CompletionStage<?> onText(WebSocket ws, CharSequence data, boolean last) {
                        String msg = data.toString();
                        bobMsgs.add(msg);
                        if (msg.startsWith("HAND_UPDATE")) {
                            startLatch.countDown();
                        }
                        if (msg.startsWith("CHAT") && msg.contains("played a combo of 2 cards!")) {
                            comboChatLatch.countDown();
                        }
                        return WebSocket.Listener.super.onText(ws, data, last);
                    }
                })
                .get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        wsAlice.sendText("JOIN Alice\n", true);
        wsBob.sendText("JOIN Bob\n", true);

        Thread.sleep(150);

        wsAlice.sendText("START\n", true);

        boolean started = startLatch.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertTrue(started, "Expected game to start");

        // Locate active game room
        GameRoom room = server.getRoomManager().getActiveRooms().values().iterator().next();
        UltimoEngine engine = room.getEngine();
        assertNotNull(engine);

        // Find current active player (Alice or Bob)
        Player current = engine.getTurnManager().getCurrentPlayer();
        WebSocket currentWs = current.getUsername().equals("Alice") ? wsAlice : wsBob;
        String nextPlayerName = engine.getTurnManager().peekNextPlayer().getUsername();

        // Setup discard top card to RED_NINE
        engine.getDiscardPile().playCard(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.NINE), CardColor.RED);

        // Give current player a matching combo: RED_NINE and BLUE_NINE
        current.getHand().clear();
        current.getHand().addCard(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.NINE));
        current.getHand().addCard(new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.NINE));
        current.getHand().addCard(new Card(CardColor.GREEN, CardValue.ONE)); // Extra card so game does not end immediately

        // Send multi-card PLAY command over WebSocket
        currentWs.sendText("PLAY RED_NINE,BLUE_NINE\n", true);

        boolean gotComboBroadcast = comboChatLatch.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        assertTrue(gotComboBroadcast, "Expected both players to receive combo chat announcement");

        Thread.sleep(150);

        // Verify top card is now BLUE_NINE (last card of played combo)
        assertEquals(new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.NINE), engine.getDiscardPile().getTopCard().orElseThrow());

        // Verify turn advanced to the next player
        assertEquals(nextPlayerName, engine.getTurnManager().getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());

        // Clean closure
        wsAlice.sendClose(WebSocket.NORMAL_CLOSURE, "Bye");
        wsBob.sendClose(WebSocket.NORMAL_CLOSURE, "Bye");
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/room/GameRoomTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.room;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.core.engine.UltimoEngine;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Card;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardColor;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.CardValue;
import pl.ultimo.core.model.Player;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ClientCommand;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ProtocolCodec;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ServerEvent;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.ClientSession;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.ConnectionListener;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.net.ServerSocket;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("GameRoom Unit and Concurrency Tests")
class GameRoomTest {

    private ExecutorService executor;
    private final List<Socket> socketsToClose = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<ServerSocket> serverSocketsToClose = new ArrayList<>();

    private record TestClient(ClientSession session, Socket clientSideSocket, BufferedReader reader, List<ServerEvent> receivedEvents) {
        public void drainEvents() {
            try {
                while (reader.ready()) {
                    String line = reader.readLine();
                    if (line != null && !line.isBlank()) {
                        receivedEvents.add(ProtocolCodec.decodeEvent(line));
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                // Ignore for drain
            }
        }
    }

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        executor.shutdownNow();
        for (Socket s : socketsToClose) {
            try {
                s.close();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        for (ServerSocket ss : serverSocketsToClose) {
            try {
                ss.close();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
    }

    private TestClient createTestClient(String sessionId, String username) throws Exception {
        ServerSocket ss = new ServerSocket(0);
        serverSocketsToClose.add(ss);

        Socket clientSocket = new Socket("localhost", ss.getLocalPort());
        Socket serverSocket = ss.accept();
        socketsToClose.add(clientSocket);
        socketsToClose.add(serverSocket);

        List<ServerEvent> received = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
        ConnectionListener dummyListener = new ConnectionListener() {
            @Override
            public void onCommandReceived(ClientSession s, ClientCommand c) {}
            @Override
            public void onSessionDisconnected(ClientSession s) {}
        };

        ClientSession session = new ClientSession(sessionId, serverSocket, dummyListener);
        session.setUsername(username);
        BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(clientSocket.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        return new TestClient(session, clientSocket, reader, received);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Players can join a room and receive ROOM_STATUS updates")
    void playersCanJoinRoom() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-1", 2);
        TestClient client1 = createTestClient("s-1", "Alice");
        TestClient client2 = createTestClient("s-2", "Bob");

        assertTrue(room.addPlayer(client1.session, "Alice"));
        assertEquals(1, room.getPlayerCount());
        assertEquals(RoomState.WAITING_FOR_PLAYERS, room.getState());

        // Second player joins room; capacity of 2 reached -> auto-starts!
        assertTrue(room.addPlayer(client2.session, "Bob"));
        assertEquals(2, room.getPlayerCount());
        assertEquals(RoomState.IN_PROGRESS, room.getState());
        assertTrue(room.isFull());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Room enforces Fog of War: players receive only their own hand")
    void roomEnforcesFogOfWar() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-fow", 2);
        TestClient alice = createTestClient("s-alice", "Alice");
        TestClient bob = createTestClient("s-bob", "Bob");

        room.addPlayer(alice.session, "Alice");
        room.addPlayer(bob.session, "Bob"); // Auto starts

        Thread.sleep(100);
        alice.drainEvents();
        bob.drainEvents();

        // Alice receives HAND_UPDATE with 7 cards
        List<ServerEvent.HandUpdate> aliceHands = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                .filter(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.HandUpdate)
                .map(e -> (ServerEvent.HandUpdate) e)
                .toList();
        assertFalse(aliceHands.isEmpty());
        assertEquals(7, aliceHands.getFirst().cards().size());

        // Bob receives HAND_UPDATE with 7 cards
        List<ServerEvent.HandUpdate> bobHands = bob.receivedEvents.stream()
                .filter(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.HandUpdate)
                .map(e -> (ServerEvent.HandUpdate) e)
                .toList();
        assertFalse(bobHands.isEmpty());
        assertEquals(7, bobHands.getFirst().cards().size());

        // Verify Bob's hand is distinct from Alice's hand
        assertNotEquals(aliceHands.getFirst().cards(), bobHands.getFirst().cards());

        // Both received OPPONENT_UPDATE
        boolean aliceGotBobCount = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                .anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.OpponentUpdate opp && opp.opponentName().equals("Bob") && opp.cardCount() == 7);
        assertTrue(aliceGotBobCount);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Chat messages are broadcast to all players in the room")
    void chatMessagesAreBroadcast() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-chat", 2);
        executor.submit(room);

        TestClient alice = createTestClient("s-1", "Alice");
        TestClient bob = createTestClient("s-2", "Bob");

        room.addPlayer(alice.session, "Alice");
        room.addPlayer(bob.session, "Bob");

        // Alice sends chat message
        room.enqueueCommand(alice.session, new ClientCommand.Chat("Hello Bob!"));

        Thread.sleep(200);
        alice.drainEvents();
        bob.drainEvents();

        boolean bobReceivedChat = bob.receivedEvents.stream()
                .anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.ChatBroadcast chat && chat.sender().equals("Alice") && chat.message().equals("Hello Bob!"));
        assertTrue(bobReceivedChat);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("If all opponents leave in an active match, remaining player wins by forfeit")
    void remainingPlayerWinsByForfeit() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-forfeit", 2);
        TestClient alice = createTestClient("s-1", "Alice");
        TestClient bob = createTestClient("s-2", "Bob");

        room.addPlayer(alice.session, "Alice");
        room.addPlayer(bob.session, "Bob"); // Starts match
        assertEquals(RoomState.IN_PROGRESS, room.getState());

        // Bob disconnects
        room.handleDisconnect(bob.session);

        Thread.sleep(100);
        alice.drainEvents();

        assertEquals(RoomState.FINISHED, room.getState());
        boolean aliceReceivedWin = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                .anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.GameOver go && go.winnerName().equals("Alice"));
        assertTrue(aliceReceivedWin);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("START command starts match manually when at least two players are in the room")
    void shouldStartMatchManuallyWithStartCommand() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-manual-start", 4);
        executor.submit(room);

        TestClient alice = createTestClient("s-1", "Alice");
        TestClient bob = createTestClient("s-2", "Bob");

        room.addPlayer(alice.session, "Alice");
        room.addPlayer(bob.session, "Bob");

        assertEquals(2, room.getPlayerCount());
        assertEquals(RoomState.WAITING_FOR_PLAYERS, room.getState());

        room.enqueueCommand(alice.session, new ClientCommand.Start());

        Thread.sleep(200);
        alice.drainEvents();
        bob.drainEvents();

        assertEquals(RoomState.IN_PROGRESS, room.getState());
        assertTrue(alice.receivedEvents.stream().anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.HandUpdate));
        assertTrue(bob.receivedEvents.stream().anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.HandUpdate));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("START command is rejected with error when fewer than two players in room")
    void shouldRejectStartCommandWithSinglePlayer() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-single", 4);
        executor.submit(room);

        TestClient alice = createTestClient("s-1", "Alice");
        room.addPlayer(alice.session, "Alice");

        assertEquals(1, room.getPlayerCount());
        assertEquals(RoomState.WAITING_FOR_PLAYERS, room.getState());

        room.enqueueCommand(alice.session, new ClientCommand.Start());

        Thread.sleep(200);
        alice.drainEvents();

        assertEquals(RoomState.WAITING_FOR_PLAYERS, room.getState());
        boolean gotError = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                .anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.ErrorEvent err && err.errorMessage().contains("Need at least 2 players"));
        assertTrue(gotError);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Player can draw a card, and if they have legal moves, manually pass their turn")
    void shouldAllowPlayerToDrawAndManuallyPassTurn() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-draw-pass", 2);
        executor.submit(room);

        TestClient alice = createTestClient("s-1", "Alice");
        TestClient bob = createTestClient("s-2", "Bob");

        room.addPlayer(alice.session, "Alice");
        room.addPlayer(bob.session, "Bob"); // Starts match, Alice's turn

        Thread.sleep(150);
        alice.drainEvents();
        bob.drainEvents();

        // Alice sends DRAW
        room.enqueueCommand(alice.session, new ClientCommand.Draw());
        Thread.sleep(150);
        alice.drainEvents();
        bob.drainEvents();

        // Verify Alice received DrawResult
        assertTrue(alice.receivedEvents.stream().anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.DrawResult));

        // If Alice still has the turn (i.e. has legal moves), test manual pass
        boolean aliceStillActive = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                .filter(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced)
                .map(e -> ((ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced) e).activePlayerName())
                .reduce((first, second) -> second)
                .map(name -> name.equals("Alice"))
                .orElse(false);

        if (aliceStillActive) {
            room.enqueueCommand(alice.session, new ClientCommand.Pass());
            Thread.sleep(150);
            alice.drainEvents();
            bob.drainEvents();

            boolean turnMovedToBob = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                    .filter(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced)
                    .map(e -> ((ServerEvent.TurnAnnounced) e).activePlayerName())
                    .reduce((first, second) -> second)
                    .map(name -> name.equals("Bob"))
                    .orElse(false);
            assertTrue(turnMovedToBob);
        }
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Auto-pass is triggered when player draws and has zero legal moves in hand")
    void shouldAutoPassWhenPlayerDrawsWithNoPlayableMoves() throws Exception {
        GameRoom room = new GameRoom("room-autopass", 2);
        executor.submit(room);

        TestClient alice = createTestClient("s-1", "Alice");
        TestClient bob = createTestClient("s-2", "Bob");

        room.addPlayer(alice.session, "Alice");
        room.addPlayer(bob.session, "Bob"); // Starts match, Alice's turn

        Thread.sleep(150);
        alice.drainEvents();
        bob.drainEvents();

        UltimoEngine engine = room.getEngine();
        assertNotNull(engine);

        // Make discard top card RED FIVE
        engine.getDiscardPile().playCard(new Card(CardColor.RED, CardValue.FIVE), CardColor.RED);

        // Clear Alice's hand and replace with single unplayable card: BLUE ZERO
        Player current = engine.getTurnManager().getCurrentPlayer();
        assertEquals("Alice", current.getUsername());
        current.getHand().clear();
        current.getHand().addCard(new Card(CardColor.BLUE, CardValue.ZERO));

        // Drain existing deck and set only unplayable card on RED FIVE (GREEN SEVEN)
        while (!engine.getDeck().isEmpty()) {
            engine.getDeck().draw();
        }
        engine.getDeck().replenishFromDiscard(List.of(new Card(CardColor.GREEN, CardValue.SEVEN)));

        // Alice draws
        room.enqueueCommand(alice.session, new ClientCommand.Draw());
        Thread.sleep(200);
        alice.drainEvents();
        bob.drainEvents();

        // Alice received DRAW_RESULT
        assertTrue(alice.receivedEvents.stream().anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.DrawResult));

        // Auto-pass broadcast was emitted to chat
        boolean gotAutoPassChat = alice.receivedEvents.stream()
                .anyMatch(e -> e instanceof ServerEvent.ChatBroadcast chat && chat.message().contains("no playable moves. Turn passed."));
        assertTrue(gotAutoPassChat);

        // Turn automatically advanced to Bob
        assertEquals("Bob", engine.getTurnManager().getCurrentPlayer().getUsername());
    }
}
```

`src/test/java/pl/ultimo/server/room/RoomManagerTest.java`:

```java
package pl.ultimo.server.room;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import pl.ultimo.protocol.ClientCommand;
import pl.ultimo.server.config.ServerConfig;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.ClientSession;
import pl.ultimo.server.net.ConnectionListener;

import java.net.ServerSocket;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

@DisplayName("RoomManager Matchmaking Unit Tests")
class RoomManagerTest {

    private RoomManager roomManager;
    private final List<Socket> socketsToClose = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<ServerSocket> serverSocketsToClose = new ArrayList<>();

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        // Room capacity of 2 for fast testing
        ServerConfig config = new ServerConfig(0, 2, 10_000, 10);
        roomManager = new RoomManager(config);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        roomManager.shutdown();
        for (Socket s : socketsToClose) {
            try {
                s.close();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        for (ServerSocket ss : serverSocketsToClose) {
            try {
                ss.close();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
    }

    private ClientSession createDummySession(String sessionId, String username) throws Exception {
        ServerSocket ss = new ServerSocket(0);
        serverSocketsToClose.add(ss);
        Socket client = new Socket("localhost", ss.getLocalPort());
        Socket server = ss.accept();
        socketsToClose.add(client);
        socketsToClose.add(server);

        ClientSession session = new ClientSession(sessionId, server, new ConnectionListener() {
            @Override
            public void onCommandReceived(ClientSession s, ClientCommand c) {}
            @Override
            public void onSessionDisconnected(ClientSession s) {}
        });
        session.setUsername(username);
        return session;
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Matchmaking places consecutive arriving players into the same open room")
    void matchmakingPlacesPlayersInSameRoom() throws Exception {
        ClientSession alice = createDummySession("s-1", "Alice");
        ClientSession bob = createDummySession("s-2", "Bob");

        GameRoom room1 = roomManager.matchmake(alice, "Alice");
        GameRoom room2 = roomManager.matchmake(bob, "Bob");

        assertSame(room1, room2, "Both players should have been matched into the exact same room");
        assertEquals(2, room1.getPlayerCount());
        assertTrue(room1.isFull());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("When a room is full, matchmaking automatically spins up a new room")
    void matchmakingCreatesNewRoomWhenFull() throws Exception {
        ClientSession alice = createDummySession("s-1", "Alice");
        ClientSession bob = createDummySession("s-2", "Bob");
        ClientSession charlie = createDummySession("s-3", "Charlie");

        GameRoom room1 = roomManager.matchmake(alice, "Alice");
        GameRoom room2 = roomManager.matchmake(bob, "Bob");
        GameRoom room3 = roomManager.matchmake(charlie, "Charlie");

        assertSame(room1, room2);
        assertNotSame(room1, room3, "Charlie should have been matched into a new room because the first is full");

        assertEquals(2, roomManager.getActiveRoomCount());
        assertEquals("room-1", room1.getRoomId());
        assertEquals("room-2", room3.getRoomId());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Disconnecting removes mapping and cleans up empty room")
    void disconnectingCleansUpRoom() throws Exception {
        ClientSession alice = createDummySession("s-1", "Alice");
        GameRoom room = roomManager.matchmake(alice, "Alice");

        assertTrue(roomManager.getRoomForSession("s-1").isPresent());
        assertEquals(1, roomManager.getActiveRoomCount());

        roomManager.handleDisconnect(alice);

        assertTrue(roomManager.getRoomForSession("s-1").isEmpty());
        assertEquals(0, roomManager.getActiveRoomCount(), "Room should be removed when empty");
    }
}

```
